# Phase 5 — UI Design Contract (Decide UI)

> Visual and interaction contract. Generated by gsd-ui-researcher, verified by gsd-ui-checker.
>
> **This file extends `03-UI-SPEC.md` and `03.1-UI-SPEC.md`; it does not replace them.** Anything not stated here is inherited unchanged: theme tokens, `space`/`size`/`radii`, type roles, Sheet engine (backdrop, drag, stacking, dismissal rules), toast, sync line, back history, FAB rules, reduced motion. All decisions come from `05-CONTEXT.md` (D-01..D-56, locked; D-38..D-44 applied in the 2026-10-15 planning-review touch-ups D-45..D-52 in the 2026-10-16 alignment to the executed Phase 4 and D-53..D-56 in the same day's revision, see the changelog at the end) and `04-CONTEXT.md` (engine codes; where a plan and the executed engine disagree, the engine code in `src/engine/decide` and `src/engine/payoff` wins). Prototype source: `FincWin United.dc.html` L955-1391 (home, steps 1-2), L1415-1723 (steps 3-5 and the action bar), L1725-1757 (past decisions), L1933-1958 (past-decision sheet), L1960-1990 and L4830-4851 (date picker), L4202-4787 (logic and strings).

**Platform note:** React Native / Expo SDK 57. No shadcn, no CSS. Styling is `StyleSheet` plus the typed theme (`useTheme()`, `textRole()`, `src/theme/*`). The CLAUDE.md fidelity rule holds: **no new colour, gradient, shadow, type size or icon set.** The prototype's Decide screens use about a dozen hex values outside the §2 palette; every one is mapped to an existing token below (Color, Deviations Register). Colours are cited by token name only; `noRawColours.test.ts` must keep passing.

**Amended 2026-10-22 and 2026-10-23 (D-70..D-99):** see "Decide UX refresh (2026-10-22)" at the end of this file. Where it conflicts with an earlier section, the refresh wins; the conflicts are listed in its R12. Re-approved by the UI checker on 2026-10-22, with its recommendations folded in (R14). R15 (Step 2, item and invest refresh, D-79..D-84) was re-approved the same day with its must-fix items folded in (R15.13). R16 (device-walkthrough refinements, D-85..D-94, 2026-10-23) was re-approved on 2026-10-23 with its must-fix items folded in (R16.16). R17 (Decide home redesign, D-95..D-99) returns the status to draft for a re-check.

**Surface in this phase:**
1. Decide tab root (quick check, Your month, Open checks, Past decisions link, Lines you will not cross).
2. The five-step check (full-screen stack, FAB and tab bar hidden).
3. Past decisions list and the decision detail sheet with estimate-vs-actual.
4. Line editors (limits, adjustments, running costs, replaced expenses, setbacks), the Go ahead confirm, the split override (built, hidden until Phase 8).
5. Two new onboarding steps: rough monthly in and out, first check.
6. Screenshot-readiness rules for the demo account (DCU-11).
7. Engine-depth surfaces (D-24..D-37): combined checks, cushion months and the edge tag, the 90-day cash path, Changes coming up, the confidence chip, one-off lines, credit terms, investing-instead views, income change, running-cost prompts, the Wait savings goal, cooling-off, the estimate gap, and currency conversion. See "Depth surfaces".

**Not in this phase:** level gating, paywall or any Pro lock UI (D-16: `canUse()` returns true, no lock chrome is drawn), household members, savings goals beyond the minimal Wait goal (D-34), a custom month-grid date picker.

---

## Design System

| Property | Value |
|----------|-------|
| Tool | none (shadcn is not applicable to React Native; no `components.json`) |
| Preset | not applicable |
| Component library | none as a package. Reuses `src/ui/Screen`, `Sheet`, `SheetHeader`, `ConfirmSheet`, `DetailSheet`, `Chip`, `Pill`, `Row`, `Text`, `TextInput`, `EmptyState`, `ToastView`, `SyncStatusLine`, `haptics.ts`, `DateField`, and the shell's FAB registration and `TabHeader`. **New primitives:** `VerdictCard`, `StateChip`, `WarningCard`, `CollapsibleSection`, `StepBar` (five tappable segments), `ChoiceCard` (radio card), `CheckRow` (checkbox row with 44px hit area), `StepSlider` (see below), `FigureStrip` (3-column caption/figure strip), `SpreadLine`, `OptionRow` (alternatives). |
| Icon library | none. Chevrons, the back arrow, checkmarks, the "x" remove glyph and the sliders are hand-drawn Views/`react-native-svg` shapes (Phase 0 convention). No icon font. |
| Font | The user's live pairing and accent via `useTheme()` / `textRole()`. Never the default pairing or default green. |
| Slider | **No new package.** `@react-native-community/slider` is not installed and is not added. `StepSlider` is built with `react-native-gesture-handler` `Gesture.Pan` plus Reanimated: 4px track `line3`, filled part `accent`, 28px thumb `surface` with 1.5px `accent` border (hit area 44px), value snaps to `step`. Accessibility: `accessibilityRole="adjustable"`, `accessibilityValue` (min/max/now/text), `increment` and `decrement` actions that move one step. A stepper alternative is not needed. |
| Notifications | `expo-notifications` (SDK-locked `~57.x`, already in the stack table) is the only dependency this phase adds; it is needed for the Wait reminder (D-10). |

## Screen map and navigation

| Screen | Kind | FAB | Tab bar | Back |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Decide home | Tab root (centre slot, never hidden by levels, 03 D-03) | "New check" / aria "Start a new affordability check" (03 D-09) | visible | tab history |
| Check flow (steps 1-5) | Full-screen stack pushed over the tab (03 D-12), `fw-push` in | hidden (03 D-10) | covered | step back, then Decide home; Android back and edge swipe do the same |
| Past decisions | Pushed detail (`fw-push`) | hidden | covered | pop |
| Decision detail, line editors, Go ahead confirm, date picker, limit editor, split override | Sheets (the app's own `Sheet`; standard backdrop, drag, dismissal rules) | hidden while open | n/a | per 03 Sheets |

- Route layout is executor discretion; the expected shape is `app/(app)/(tabs)/decide.tsx`, `app/(app)/decide/check/[id].tsx`, `app/(app)/decide/past.tsx`, and `app/(app)/setup/rough.tsx`, `app/(app)/setup/first-check.tsx`.
- **A check autosaves on every edit** (debounced, written through the offline write queue). Leaving the flow by back, Android back, edge swipe or the "Save and close" button never prompts and never loses work. "Save and close" and returns to Decide home with the toast "Saved". Abandoning is only done from the open-check row (below).
- Opening a check from Decide home lands on **step 1 for a draft, step 3 for a check that has a price and payment method** (it answers first, D-05).
- **Step bar jumping (D-08):** segments up to the furthest step already reached are tappable; later segments are inert (`accessibilityState={{ disabled: true }}`). Jumping to step 3 or later needs a price above zero and a payment method chosen; otherwise the tap lands on the first incomplete step and the bottom hint explains why (copy below). A `missing` plan does not block step 3: the verdict card explains it.
- Focus moves to the step title on every step change (03 D-20 pattern).

## Spacing Scale

Reuse `src/theme/layout.ts` `space` and `size` exactly. **No new spacing token is added.** The prototype's 14/12/13/11/9/7 gaps are normalised onto existing tokens.

| Use | Value (token) |
|---|---|
| Card side margin | 16 (`space.cardPad`) |
| Vertical gap between cards | 18 (`space.groupGap`) on Decide home, as shipped (amended 2026-10-22; was 16); 12 (`space.gapMd`) between blocks in the check flow (prototype 12/14 normalised) |
| Card inner padding | 20 (`space.cardPadLg`) |
| Warning stack gap | 8 (`space.gapSm`) |
| Gap between rows inside a card, chip gaps, icon gaps | 12 (`space.gapMd`) / 8 (`space.gapSm`) |
| Row separator | 1px `fill2` (inside white cards), padding `space.rowPadDense` (13) vertical |
| Tab-root header | `space.screenH` (22) sides, top `insets.top + 21` (`space.headerExtra`), as 03 |
| Flow header (sticky) | sides `space.detailHeaderPadH` (18), top `insets.top + space.detailHeaderPadTop` (15), bottom `space.detailHeaderPadBottom` (12); back circle `size.backCompact` (36, 44 hit); step bar 12px below the title row |
| Bottom action bar (flow) | padding 12 (`space.gapMd`) sides 16 (`space.cardPad`); bottom `Math.max(insets.bottom, space.gapMd)`; buttons 48px high (>= 44); 1px `line1` top border, `canvas` fill |
| Touch targets | 44 (`space.touchMin`) everywhere; checkboxes, the "x" remove glyph, step segments and slider thumbs reach it through `hitSlop` |
| Last card bottom space | 30 (`space.sheetPadBottom`) before the action bar |

**Radii (normalised onto the declared set):** outer cards 26 (`radii.card`), including the prototype's 24 and 22 cards; inner tiles and inputs 16 (prototype 14/16/18/20 normalised); pills and chips `radii.pill`. Nothing else.

**Normalised onto tokens (no longer exceptions):** the prototype's 21 (verdict card), 19/20 ("What gets written"), 9/11 (delta strip), 14/15 (option rows), 5 (scope toggle) and 16/18 (warning cards) become `cardPadLg` (20), `cardPadLg`, `gapSm`/`gapMd`, `gapMd`/`cardPad`, 4 (matching the Cash | Credit toggle) and `cardPad` (16). Row padding in the option rows and lists is `rowPadDense` (13).

**Declared prototype-literal exceptions (Decide):** the 4px step segment height; the 19px/9px radio ring and dot; the 20px checkbox visual (hit 44); the 4px toggle track padding (equal to the shipped segmented control precedent); the 6px/8px state-chip padding is taken from the shipped `Chip`/`Pill` rather than the prototype. Each is a one-off and none becomes a token. **Chart and marker geometry (declared exceptions, reason: drawing coordinates inside `react-native-svg`, not layout spacing):** chart padding 36 left / 8 right / 8 top / 22 bottom (room for y labels and x labels); chart height 148 (about a 2.2:1 plot at 330 wide); the 10px combined-switch state dot; the 6px lowest-day marker and 5px hollow marker; the 2px line weight. They apply only to `CashPathChart` and `NetWorthChart` and the switch dot, and are not reused elsewhere. **Derived geometry (declared 2026-10-22, R14):**
- the InfoButton `hitSlop` of 14 on each side, which is (44 − 16) / 2 and takes the 16px glyph to the 44 target;
- the bullet and dot `marginTop`, which is (line height − dot size) / 2, about 7 for a 6px dot on an `s13_5` line.

Both are computed from tokens and are not spacing values.

**Above-the-fold budget at 402 x 874 (DCU-11), default text size, iPhone 16 Pro insets (top 62, bottom 34).** Derived from real line counts (meta 12.5 x 1.4 = 17.5 per line, `s13_5` 13.5 x 1.5 = 20 per line, `heading` 26 x 1.16 = 30 per line, `body` 15 x 1.5 = 22.5). Budgets apply at default text size; above 1.3x text the caps on lines lift and the screen scrolls.

| Screen | Required visible without scrolling | Budget (y in dp from the top) |

Quick-check footer row: with 2 or more open checks it also holds the combined-checks pill and becomes 44px high (+26): in the tallest Credit state the chip, size label, line and monthly figure still end at or above y = 543 and the footer row at or above y = 594; the buttons and "+ Tax, fees" stay below the fold as already stated.

Result placement rule: inside the quick-check card the result block sits **above** the Credit controls and the extras row (order: amount pill, toggle, result, Credit controls, "+ Tax, fees", buttons). It recomputes live as APR, duration, tax or fees are edited below it, so editing never hides the answer. The "Still short before any interest..." sentence is merged into the result line (3-line cap), not stacked after it.

If a budget fails on device, trim in this order: body copy line count (shorten, never drop the basis), then card padding to `space.cardPad`. Never go below 44px targets, never shrink type below the scale.

## Typography

Closed scale from `src/theme/typography.ts`; **no new size or weight.** Decide inherits the closed §2 set unchanged: 8 sizes (10.5, 11.5, 12.5, 13, 13.5, 15, 16, 26) and 3 weights (500, 600, 700); nothing is added. The prototype's off-scale sizes map to the nearest declared role (the 03 Home precedent: 30 maps to `heading`, 17 to `sheetTitle`).

| Role | Size / weight / line height | Used for in Decide |
|---|---|---|
| `heading` | 26 / 700 / 1.16, ls -.01em | Tab-root title "Can I afford it"; **verdict card title** (capped 1.3x); the Final price figure; the Cheaper-one price; the Put-aside-a-month figure; the price input; the "Left" figure in Your month when emphasised |
| `sheetTitle` | 16 / 600 / 1.2, ls -.01em | Step title in the flow header; option and outcome names; sheet titles; money figures in strips (prototype 17-22); the name input; open-check and past-decision names are `body` instead |
| `body` | 15 / 500 / 1.5 | Open-check names, row names in lists, line items, payment-card figures (prototype 16.5) |
| `label` | 13 / 600 / 1.4 | Section labels ("Lines you will not cross"), chip text, button text, links, "Check" |
| `s13_5` | 13.5 / 500 / 1.5 | Verdict body, warning body, quick-check result line, helper paragraphs that carry a conclusion |
| `meta` (12.5 via the `label` role with size override, the 03 sync-line precedent) | 12.5 / 600 / 1.4 (500 for descriptive sub-lines) | Sub-lines, row descriptions, table notes, footnotes (prototype 11-12.5) |
| `eyebrow` | 11.5 / 600, ls `.11em` upper-cased (the prototype's section-head tracking; Home uses .1em for captions, the section-head value is kept) | Section heads inside cards ("The month, line by line"), "STEP 2 OF 5", size label above the verdict title |
| `tabLabel` | 10.5 / 600 | State chips, column captions in figure strips ("On the day", "A month", "Kept"), status chips in lists |

- Catalogue stores sentence case; styles upper-case eyebrows (03 precedent). The accessible label stays sentence case.
- Display figures that cap at 1.3x (`maxFontSizeMultiplier`): verdict title, Final price, strip figures, "Left", Cheaper-one price. Everything else scales to 2x and wraps or stacks.
- **Stacking rule:** every 3-column strip (`FigureStrip`, Your month, plan card) stacks to one column (caption left, figure right) above 1.3x text scale. The alternatives rows do the same.
- Line heights come from `textRole()`; the prototype's text-wrap:pretty has no RN equivalent and is dropped.

## Color

Tokens only from `src/theme/tokens.ts` and the live accent. **Prototype hex to token mapping** (every Decide hex not in §2):

| Prototype value | Where used | Token used instead |
|---|---|---|
| `#14150F` verdict/Your-month/trade/options card, selected radio card | dark cards | `ink` fill, `surface` text, `inkSoft1` for secondary text |
| `#5A2318` verdict card for "no" | verdict card | `danger` fill (`#B4472A`), `surface` text; white on `#B4472A` is 5.4:1 (AA). Chip stays `dangerTint2` bg / `danger` text. Recorded as a deviation |
| `#2A2B23`, `#22231B`, `#3A3B31`, `#35362C` (dark-card inner chips and rows) | basis chips, option rows, action chips on dark cards | `surface` at 0.12 opacity over the `ink` card (an opacity derivation, same method as the pending bar and backdrop), selected variant `surface` fill with `ink` text |
| `#EAF1EC` / `#1B4D3E` "fits" chip, pass dots, spare figures | state chip, check dots, positive figures | chip: `accent` at `ACCENT_TINT_OPACITY` over `fill1` with `accent` text (follows the picked accent, 03.1 Chip method); dots and figures: `accent` |
| `#F6EFE2` / `#8A5A1B` "needs adjustment", "waiting" chips | state chip | `categoryTint.Business` fill (`#F4EFE3`, an existing tint) with `warn1` text |
| `#FBF3E6` bg, `#EFE1C9` border, `#C08A2E` dot, `#7A5312` text (soft warning card) | `WarningCard` soft | bg `categoryTint.Business`, border 1.5px `line2`, dot `warn1`, title `warn1`, body `inkDim` |
| `#F6EAE5` bg, `#EAD4CC` border, `#B4472A` dot, `#8A2D1A` text (hard warning card) | `WarningCard` hard | bg `dangerTint3`, border 1.5px `dangerTint2`, dot `danger`, title `danger`, body `inkDim` (`danger` on `dangerTint3` is 4.6:1, AA) |
| `#8FD6A8`, `#E0A088` (value colours on dark cards) | option values, "Left" when negative | positive: `accentTint1`; negative: `dangerTint2`. Sign and words carry meaning, never colour alone |
| `#EFEDE6` neutral chip ("Draft", "Missing terms", "Passed") | chips | `fill1` fill, `inkDim` text |
| `#F6F4EE` input/tile fill | inputs, stat tiles | `fill6` |
| `#F5F2EB` separators | row separators | `fill2` |
| `#E5E2D7` unreached step segment, `#D9D5C8` unchecked box border | step bar, checkboxes | `line3`, `line4` |
| `#C6C2B6` chevrons, radio ring | | `inkSoft3` |
| `rgba(20,21,15,.42)` date picker backdrop | date sheet | the shared `ink` at 0.38 sheet backdrop (03 D-23) |
| `box-shadow` on sheets and floating cards | | none (only `shadows.card` on white cards) |

| Role | Token | Usage |
|---|---|---|
| Dominant (60%) | `canvas` | Screen and flow background, action bar |
| Secondary (30%) | `surface` + `shadows.card`; `fill1`/`fill6` for inputs, tiles, unselected chips | White cards, sheets |
| Dark emphasis | `ink` (and `danger` for the `no` verdict card) | Verdict card, Your month, trade line, options list, selected radio card, selected segmented control. No more than **two dark cards visible at once** on a screen |
| Accent (10%) | `accent` | **Reserved for exactly:** the single primary button per screen (Check, Next, Go ahead / Save the plan / Record as passed, Bring your history); text links ("+ Purchase", "+ Investment", "Edit", "+ Add a rate", "Show"); checked checkbox fill; the slider fill; selected-chip text and tint (shipped `Chip`); the "fits" state chip and pass dots; spare/positive figures; the goal progress fill (D-34); the combined-checks switch dot when on; the "Strong basis" chip and the "Income" tag (both accent at `ACCENT_TINT_OPACITY` over `fill1`); the ledger "better" dots and pass dots; the focus ring; **added 2026-10-22 (R14):**
- the "Show details" / "Hide details" link text;
- the add row's "+" glyph and its "Add a change coming up" label;
- the "Check an investment" link;
- the "What gets written" bullets;
- the "Better" direction tag (accent at `ACCENT_TINT_OPACITY` over `fill1`) in Where it lands. **Not** for: tab selection, backdrop, toast, verdict card fill, the step bar (that is `ink`), dark-card controls |
| Warning | `warn1` on `categoryTint.Business` | Soft warnings, "Needs adjustment" and "Waiting" chips, suppressed-row reasons, "Changed" markers |
| Destructive / loss | `danger` | Hard warnings, "Doesn’t fit right now" chip and card, negative figures, "Over"/"Short" values, Remove. Always paired with words or a sign |

- Focus ring: 2px `accent`, 2px offset (BUILD-PROMPT §2).
- The step bar's reached segments are `ink`, unreached `line3`. Selected radio cards are `ink` with a `surface` ring and dot.
- `StateChip` map (used for verdict, open-check rows, past decisions):

| State | Fill / text | Words |
|---|---|---|
| fits | `accent`@.12 over `fill1` / `accent` | "It fits" |
| adjust | `categoryTint.Business` / `warn1` | "Needs adjustment" |
| no | `dangerTint2` / `danger` | "Doesn’t fit right now" (full check), "Not from cash" or "Not from credit" (quick check) |
| missing | `fill1` / `inkDim` | "Missing terms" or "Card limits apply" |
| draft | `fill1` / `inkDim` | "Draft" |
| bought / waiting / passed | accent-tint / `categoryTint.Business`+`warn1` / `fill1`+`inkDim` | "Bought" / "Waiting" / "Passed" |

## Decide home (tab root)

Order, top to bottom (deviation: the quick check moves above Your month so the public promise is the first thing and its result stays above the fold, see budget):

1. **Header** (tab-root header, 03 D-14): eyebrow "DECIDE" (`eyebrow`, `inkMuted`), title "Can I afford it" (`heading`), 42px avatar at the trailing edge, then a one-line subtitle (`s13_5`, `inkMuted`, ellipsis): "Judged on your real accounts, not the price tag." Scrolls into the compact header like every tab.
2. **Quick check** (below).
3. **Your month** card.
4. **Open checks** card.
5. **Past decisions** row card (a single `Row` with chevron; sub-line "{n} decisions" or "Nothing decided yet").
6. **Lines you will not cross** card.
7. **Sources note** (`fill1` card, `meta` `inkDim`): "{n} months of logged income, {n} expense lines this month, {n} debts and {n} accounts. Every figure here rescores when they change in Activity." Before a full month exists: "Rough figures you entered. A statement import or one full logged month replaces them."

No pull-to-refresh on Decide (03). The Decide tab shows the sync line like every tab.

### Quick check (DCU-01, D-01, D-02, D-22, D-23)

D-23 supersedes the D-21 "spread line only when cash does not cover it" layout. **Every quick check carries the same inputs, in cash or credit mode, and both modes are scored by `assess()`.** One card holds the inputs and the result.

**Layout, top to bottom (one `surface` card, `radii.card`, `space.cardPadLg`, 12 gaps; replaces the old separate pill + result card):**

1. **Amount pill:** (`fill1`, 56px high, `radii.pill`, left padding 16) one text field, placeholder "Something and a price — car 14500", aria "Quick check", return key "Go". A **"Check it"** button inside it (accent fill, `surface` `label` text, **44px high**). Parser is the prototype's: the first number is the price, the remaining text is the name (first letter upper-cased, empty gives "Unnamed"). No number: toast "A figure is needed — “car 14500” works". The card appears after the first successful parse and then **recomputes live on every edit of any field**.
2. **Cash | Credit toggle:** a two-segment control (`fill1` track, `radii.pill`, padding 4, segments 44px high; selected `ink` fill with `surface` text, unselected `inkMuted`). Starts on **Cash**. `radiogroup` with aria "Pay with", each segment `role="radio"` with `checked`; `selectionAsync` haptic on change.
3. **Result block** (separated by a 1px `fill2` rule; **placed directly under the toggle so the answer stays above the fold**):
   - Row A: item name (`body`, 600 weight override, one line ellipsis) at left, `StateChip` at right.
   - Row B: **size label** (D-22) as a `tabLabel` chip (`fill1` / `inkDim`): "Out of this month's spare" / "Out of savings" / "Needs spreading".
   - Row C: the one line (`s13_5`, `inkDim`), capped at 3 lines.
   - Row D (**Credit only**): **monthly amount**, `heading` figure (capped 1.3x) with caption (`meta`, `inkMuted`) "a month for {n} months"; the figure shows cents (D-54). **At 0% the caption is exactly "a month for {n} months, before any interest"** (one `meta` line, at most 43 characters) and never carries the payoff suffix below (UI-check F4). `{n}` is always the term the user picked. Level payments round up to the cent (04 D-64), so a small amount can be paid off before the term ends; then, above 0% and with something financed, the caption is "a month for {n} months · paid off in month {m}" (still one `meta` line, at most 46 characters, so the fold budget is unchanged). The chosen duration chip stays selected (D-45).
   - Row E (**Cash that does not cover it**): the gap line and a secondary pill **"See it on credit"** (`fill1`, `ink` text, 44px). It flips the toggle to Credit, keeps the amount, tax and fees, leaves APR at 0 and selects 12 months, then recomputes.
   - Footer (`meta`, `inkFaint`, one line): "Worked out from the figures you entered."
4. **Credit-only block** (rendered only in Credit mode, `fw-rise`, below the result):
   - **APR field:** a pill input (`fill1`, `inputMode="decimal"`, width ~72, suffix "%"), label `meta` "Annual rate". It **starts at 0**. Directly under it, `meta` `inkFaint`: **"before any interest"** whenever the value is 0 (not only while untouched, so a 0 can never read as a quoted rate); above 0 it reads "a year". The 0 is user-editable input, not a rate the engine supplies (04 D-07).
   - **Duration chips:** five shipped `Chip`s "12", "24", "36", "48", "60" under the label "Over {n} months"; **12 selected** on first show; 44px high; one row at 402 below 1.3x text scale, wrapping to two rows above it.
5. **"+ Tax, fees" row (Cash and Credit):** a collapsed row (44px, `label`, `accent` text "+ Tax, fees", `accessibilityState.expanded`). Expanded: **"Sales tax"** (`%` pill input, **empty, no default rate**, placeholder "The rate") and **"Fees"** (money pill input, empty). Both add to the day-one price. Collapsed summary shows "+{amt}" once either is non-zero. The fold budget excludes the expanded state.
6. **Buttons:** "Run the full check" (`ink` pill, `surface` text, 44+ high, flex 1) and **"Clear the check"** (`fill1` pill).

**One-line copy (declarative; amounts are the user's own):**

| Mode / verdict | Chip | Line |
|---|---|---|
| Cash, fits | It fits | "Paid outright today your cash lands at {cashAfter}, above your {cushion} cushion, and the month still closes with {left} spare." |
| Cash, adjust, cash below cushion | Needs adjustment | "Paid outright today your cash lands at {cashAfter}, {short} under your {cushion} cushion. The month still closes." |
| Cash, adjust, month short | Needs adjustment | "Cash covers it, but the month would end {short} short." (or the D-35 wording: "The month closes only if {x} a month of investing pauses.") |
| Cash, no | Not from cash | "Not from cash: {short} short of your cushion." then "See it on credit". Without a cash warning (the precedence is: cash below 0, then the month, then a hard cash dip): month short "Cash covers it, but the month would end {short} short."; a hard dip alone "Paid outright, your cash runs out before money comes in." (both still offer "See it on credit") |
| Cash, adjust, led by another warning | Needs adjustment | The line follows the warning that leads (largest shortfall): `cash_dip` "Paid outright, cash dips {x} under your cushion by payday."; `dsr` "Debt payments would reach {pct} of money in." (no money in: "No money comes in, so any debt payment is over your debt line."); `owe` "Unsecured debt would pass a year's income."; `inv` the pause line above. Every leading code has a line |
| Credit, led by `cash_dip` | Needs adjustment / Not from credit | soft "{payment} a month; cash dips {x} under your cushion by payday."; hard "{payment} a month; your cash runs out before money comes in." |
| Credit, fits | It fits | "{payment} a month for {n} months. The month closes with {left} spare." |
| Credit, adjust or no | Needs adjustment / Not from credit | "{payment} a month for {n} months. The month is {short} short." or the `assess()` line: "Debt payments would reach {pct} of money in." / "Unsecured debt would pass a year's income." With no money in on the basis (the ratio cannot be worked out and any debt payment is over the line, 04 ME-05): "No money comes in, so any debt payment is over your debt line." (D-50) |
| Credit at 0%, still short | Needs adjustment / Not from credit | "{payment} a month for {n} months, before any interest. Still {short} short, so no rate makes it fit." (one merged sentence; upper-bound rule kept) |
| Credit at 0%, fits | It fits | adds "Any rate adds to this." |
| First read (no full month) | the state's chip | the same sentences from `firstRead()`, plus the basis line "First read, from the figures you gave." |

The "no" chip is "Not from cash" in Cash mode and **"Not from credit"** in Credit mode: the quick check judges one payment route, so it never says "Doesn’t fit right now" (the full check does). The prototype's hardcoded "Spread over 48 months ... 9.4%" sentence is removed (D-02, 04 D-07).

**"Run the full check" (D-23)** carries **every field**: name, price, mode, APR, duration, sales tax and fees. Cash maps to "Paid in full"; Credit maps to "Deposit and loan" with deposit 0, the entered rate and the chosen term; tax and fees become step-2 adjustment lines ("Sales tax", "Fees", both "Paid on the day"). A carried 0% shows "before any interest" in step 2 so it is not read as a quoted rate.

**Card-path guard (compliance):** the Credit block says "a month", "Annual rate" and "Over {n} months"; it never says "loan offer", "apply", "approved", "from {rate}%", "pre-qualified", or shows a representative APR. It is arithmetic on the user's own numbers. A one-line `meta` footer in the result: "Worked out from the figures you entered."

**Accessibility (toggle, chips, extras):**
- Toggle: `radiogroup` "Pay with"; segments announce "Cash, selected, 1 of 2" / "Credit, 2 of 2". Switching mode announces the new result through the card's polite live region.
- Duration chips are a `radiogroup` "Over how many months" (each `radio`, `checked`, label "{n} months"). The APR and tax fields are labelled "Annual rate, percent" and "Sales tax, percent"; "before any interest" is the APR field's hint.
- "+ Tax, fees" exposes `expanded`. The result group reads "{chip}. {size}. {line}. {payment} a month for {n} months."
- "See it on credit" has the visible words in its label ("See it on credit, keeps the amount").
- All targets 44px; selection is never colour alone (fill plus `checked`).

**States:** loading (skeleton bar in the result position, `fill1`, busy label "Checking"); no figures at all (no full month and no rough figures): the card shows state chip "Missing terms", line "There is nothing to judge this against yet.", and buttons "Add rough figures" (accent) / "Clear the check"; error (snapshot failed): `EmptyState` style inline card, heading "Your figures didn't load", body "A check needs your latest figures.", button "Try again". The result clears on tab change.

### Your month (dark card)

`ink` card, `radii.card`, padding `space.cardPadLg`, no shadow.

- Header row: eyebrow "Your month" (`inkSoft1`), then a steadiness chip, then a small pill "Activity" (`surface`@.12 over `ink`, `surface` text) that switches to the Activity tab.
- Steadiness chip words (`tabLabel`): "Steady", "Variable", "Volatile" (chip colours from the StateChip map: accent-tint, `Business`+`warn1`, `dangerTint2`+`danger`) and **"Not enough history yet"** (`fill1`/`inkDim`) when engine steadiness is `insufficient` (under 3 full months, 04 D-12).
- Three columns **Comes in / Goes out / Left** (`FigureStrip`, caption `tabLabel` `inkSoft1`, figure `sheetTitle`; "Left" in `dangerTint2` when negative, with the minus sign).
- Basis line (`meta`, `inkSoft2`):
  - Steady: "{n} months of income within {pct} of each other, so the check uses the average." Amended 2026-10-22 (review WR-D04 / WR-B09): the prototype's "The average is safe to plan on." was an assurance about planning, not a record of the data.
  - No money in (3 or more full months, all with 0 income; the engine reports Volatile, 04 LO-01): "No money in is logged in these {n} months, so every check is judged on that." It replaces the not-steady line, so the card never reads "ranging $0 to $0" (D-50).
  - Not steady: "{n} months ranging {worst} to {best}. Affordability is judged on the worst month unless you say otherwise."
  - Under 3 full months: "Fewer than 3 full months so far, so this uses your lowest month for money in and your highest for spending."
- Basis chips (only when 3 or more full months exist): "Worst · {amt}" and "Average · {amt}", a two-segment control, selected `surface`/`ink`, unselected `surface`@.12/`inkSoft2`, 44px high. Below 3 months the control is replaced by the fixed text above.
- **Before a full month exists (D-04):** the card reads "From the figures you gave" in the eyebrow slot, shows the rough in and out, "Left" is in minus out, and an "Edit" text link (`surface`, 44 hit) opens the rough-figures sheet (two money fields; "Cancel" / "Save figures"). The moment one full month exists the card switches to the logged version and the verdict basis lines read "Now from your logged months".

### Open checks (DCU-03, DCU-04)

- Heading row above the card: eyebrow "Open checks" at left; "+ Purchase" and "+ Investment" at right (`label`, `accent`, 44 hit, 8 gap; at large text they wrap under the heading).
- White list card, `radii.card`, `overflow hidden`. Each row (padding `space.rowPad` 15 vertical, 18 horizontal), left to right: a body block (flex): name (`body`, one line ellipsis) + `StateChip` on the same line; sub-line (`meta`, `inkFaint`, up to 2 lines); a chevron (7px rotated borders, `inkSoft3`); and a 44x44 **remove** target (visual 28 circle `fill6`, "x" glyph `inkMuted`, aria "Abandon this check").
- Sub-line: "{price} · {method} · leaves {left} a month" (for a draft with no method: "{price} quoted · no payment method yet"). A check that has been saved shows " · saved".
- **Changed-since-you-looked marker (D-15):** verdicts are recomputed each time shown, never stored. If the freshly computed state differs from the last state the user saw, the sub-line starts with "Changed since you looked" in `warn1` 600 followed by " · was {previous state words}", e.g. "Changed since you looked · was It fits". Opening the check clears the marker (stores the state seen). The marker is text, never colour alone.
- **Abandon (DCU-04):** tapping remove deletes the open check at once (no confirm) and shows the toast "Check abandoned — nothing was written" with **Undo** (restores it, 02.x undo stack). Destructive timing (about 6s) applies.
- **Empty:** the card shows a centred block (padding 22 x 18): "No checks open" (`label`), "Start one above, or type an item and a price in the quick check." (`meta`, `inkMuted`). The prototype's "Restore both" is removed.

### Lines you will not cross (D-14)

White card, `radii.card`, padding `space.cardPadLg`. Heading `label` `inkMuted`; sub-copy (`meta`, `inkFaint`): "These decide what counts as affordable. Tap any of them to change it." Five tappable rows (44+ high, separators `fill2`), each: name (`s13_5`) + description (`meta`) at left, value (`body`) at right, pressed opacity `PRESSED_OPACITY`.

| Row | Description | Blank (default) shows |
|---|---|---|
| Cash never below | "The cushion no purchase may break" | value = 3 months of your costs in money, tag "default" |
| Debt payments at most | "Share of money in, all debts counted" | "36%" tag "default" |
| Unsecured debt at most | "A multiple of your average monthly income" | "12 ×" tag "default" |
| Investing at least | "A month, protected from purchases" | what you invest now, tag "default" |
| Saving at least | "A month, into goals" | what you save now, tag "default" |

The "default" tag is a `tabLabel` `fill1`/`inkDim` pill, so a user can see which lines are theirs and which the engine fills in. Tapping opens a short-form edit sheet (`Sheet`, `SheetHeader` with "Cancel" / "Save limit"): title = row name; one numeric field (money prefix, `%` suffix, or `×` suffix as fits); an info line (e.g. "You hold {cash} across {n} accounts today." / "Your automatic contributions come to {amt} a month."); a text link "Use the default" that blanks the line; dirty-state discard rule per 03 D-26. Limits are one set per user and apply to every check; open checks recompute against the new values.

---

## Check flow shell

**Sticky flow header** (`canvas`, 1px `line1` bottom border when scrolled): back circle (36, `fill1`, hand-drawn chevron) with aria "Back"; a text block: eyebrow "STEP {n} OF 5" (`eyebrow`, `inkMuted`) over the step title (`sheetTitle`, one line ellipsis); the **step bar** 12px below: five equal segments, 4px high, 5px gap, reached = `ink`, unreached = `line3`, each segment with a 44px vertical hit area and aria "Step {n}, {title}". Investment checks use the alternate titles from the prototype ("The investment", "Amount and schedule").

Step titles: 1 "The item", 2 "Price and how you pay", 3 "Impact", 4 "Alternatives", 5 "The decision".

**Scope toggle (D-18):** a segmented control (`fill1` track, radius pill, padding 4, segments 44px high, selected `ink`/`surface`, unselected `inkMuted`) "Just me" / "{household name}" under the header, with a `meta` share line ("Split evenly across {n} people — your share is {pct}." / "Counted entirely against your own money."). **Rendered only when the household has more than one member; until Phase 8 it never renders**, and the demo and screenshots run in personal scope. The built split override sits beside it: a `Row` "Split for this purchase" opening a `Sheet` with four choices (Even, By weight, Set amounts, All mine) as radio rows with "Cancel" / "Save split"; "Set amounts" shows one money field per member.

**Bottom action bar** (pinned, not scrolled; deviation from the prototype, where it sits at the end of the scroll): optional hint line above the buttons (`meta`, `inkFaint`), then **"Save and close"** (`fill1` pill, `ink` text; shows "Saved" for a moment) and the primary button (flex 1, accent fill, `surface` text, `label`). Primary labels: step 1 "Next: Price", step 2 "Next: Impact", step 3 "Next: Alternatives", step 4 "Next: Decide", step 5 "Go ahead" / "Save the plan" / "Record as passed" per the chosen outcome, or **"Pick an outcome"** (disabled: `line3` fill, `inkDim` text, `accessibilityState.disabled`). Hints: step 3 "Nothing here changes your balances. Step five is where anything gets written."; step 5 with Go ahead and a non-fitting verdict "This breaks a line you set. You can still log it — the record notes it."; step 5 otherwise "Waiting and passing leave every balance exactly as it is."; step 1 with no price "A price comes first." Keyboard: the bar rides above the keyboard; scroll views use `keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"`.

**Reveal and haptics:** arriving on step 3, the verdict card enters with `fw-rise` (`DURATION.entrance`); one haptic fires on that first reveal per visit: `hapticSuccess` (`notificationAsync` Success) for "fits", `hapticWarning` (`notificationAsync` Warning) for "needs adjustment", "doesn’t fit right now" and "missing". Committing Go ahead or Wait fires `impactAsync` Medium (`hapticMedium`); Pass fires no haptic beyond the selection one. Choosing a payment method, a chip or a step segment fires `selectionAsync` (`hapticSelection`). Haptics are suppressed under reduced motion only if the OS haptic setting is off (03 rule). No sound.

---

## Step 1: The item (DCU-02, DCU-08)

Cards, top to bottom (`fw-rise` in on step change):

1. **Item card:** kind chips "A purchase" / "An investment" (two flex chips, 44 high, selected `ink`/`surface`); label "What it is" (investment: "What you are putting money into", `meta` `inkMuted`); name input (`sheetTitle`, bottom border 1.5px `line1`); label "Price you have been quoted" (investment: "Amount a month" / "Amount"); price row: currency symbol (`sheetTitle`, `inkFaint`) + numeric input (`heading`, `inputMode="decimal"`); optional source input placeholder "Who quoted it" (investment "Where it is held"); and an **item type** `Row` with chevron (opens the existing `Dropdown`): "General", "Vehicle", "Rent or bill", "Loan repayment", "Tax", "Insurance premium" (04 D-14). It is pre-filled from the name by the prototype regex as a suggestion only (the row shows "Suggested from the name" in `meta` until the user touches it). The type decides card eligibility and the loan's secured flag.
2. **When and why card:** label "When you need it" (investment: "Start on"). **Quick-offset chips above the date row** (D-13): "In a week", "In a month", "In 3 months" (three flex chips, 44 high; the one that equals the current date is selected). Below them the existing `DateField` row (`minDate` = today, so past days are not offered; native picker, inline in a `Sheet` on iOS, system dialog on Android) showing the long date (e.g. "Sun 15 Nov 2026") and an away sub-line (`meta`): "Today", "Tomorrow", "In {n} days", "In {n} month(s)". The "Change" affordance is the row chevron. A past date can never be committed: a stored date earlier than today (an old open check) is shown in `danger` with the sub-line "This date has passed" and the Next button is disabled until it is changed. Then label "Why" and a 2-row text area (`fill6`, radius 16, placeholder "Why this, and why now") and the note "Kept on the record so the check never treats doing nothing as free."
3. **Expenses it replaces** (purchases only): header "Expenses it replaces" with the sum at right (`meta`, `accent`: "−{amt}/mo" or "none yet"); explainer "Lines you already pay that this purchase ends. They come off the monthly cost, so the impact is the net change."; `CheckRow`s (22px visual checkbox, 44 hit; name `s13_5` + source `meta`; amount `body`; "x" remove); button "+ Add an expense it replaces" (`fill1` pill, 44 high) opens the line editor sheet.

**Line editor sheet (shared by every list in the flow):** title per context ("Add a cost, fee or trade-in", "Add a running cost", "Add an expense it replaces", "Add a setback to test"); fields Name, Amount (money), Note or source (optional); adjustments add a two-option control "Adds to the price" / "Takes off the price" and a toggle "Paid on the day"; setbacks add the kind choice (Once, Income drops, Job or client loss, Invoices pay late) with months and percentage fields; "Cancel" / "Save line"; Delete is the row "x" in the list (undo toast), not in the sheet. Dirty discard per 03 D-26.

---

## Step 2: Price and how you pay

1. **Price card:** label "Advertised" (investment "Amount") + "Edit" link (`accent`, 44 hit, jumps to step 1) and the figure (`sheetTitle`); adjustment rows (checkbox, name + note, "On the day" tag pill `fill1` when paid on the day, amount signed, "x"); "+ Add a cost, fee or trade-in" pill; footer rule (1.5px `ink` top border): "Final price" (investment "Amount committed"), math line "{n} of {m} lines counted", figure (`heading`).
2. **Section head** (`eyebrow`) "How you pay" (investment "How you put it in") + sub-copy "The same thing, four ways. Everything downstream is worked out on the one you pick." (investment sub-copy per the prototype, L4351).
3. **Payment `ChoiceCard`s** (radio group; `role="radio"`, `accessibilityState.checked`): unselected `surface` + 1.5px `line1` border; selected `ink` fill, `surface` text, `inkSoft1` for captions. Card: ring + dot, method name (`sheetTitle`), optional flag chip at right; a `FigureStrip` ("On the day", "A month", "Total paid"; figures `body`); a `meta` note. Methods: **Paid in full**, **Deposit and loan**, **Instalment plan**, **On a card**; investment: **Monthly contribution**, **Lump sum**. Flag chips (`tabLabel`): "Rate needed" (no APR entered; the plan is `missing`), "Not accepted" (reason `card_not_accepted`), "Part on the card" (capped vehicle: the part above the dealer cap is a day-one cash cost, 04 D-18), "Below the price" (instalments that add up to less than they must cover, reason `instalment_total_below_price`, D-49). A strip figure the engine returns as null (a card minimum's "Total paid") reads "Endless" when it never clears and "Not within 50 years" when it is still open at the 600-month cap, never a number or "—". Notes: paid in full "Leaves {cash} in cash. No interest, nothing owed after."; loan "{dep} down, {n} months at {apr}%. Interest over the term is {amt}." (`{n}` is the term typed; with no APR: "The rate on the quote to score this."); instalment "{n} × {pay} as quoted. That implies {amt} on top of the price."; card "At {rate}%, the rate on your card record." (card with no rate: "Type the rate on your card to score months or minimum plans."; paid in full next statement needs no rate, 04 D-07). No rate is ever pre-filled by the app.
4. **Method panels (one visible at a time):**
   - **Loan:** Deposit (money pill input + `StepSlider` 0..price, step 100, two stat tiles "Interest saved" / "Cash left after"); Term (numeric pill input "mo", chips 12/24/36/48/60/84, up to 600 months, note "At {n} months you pay {pay} a month and {int} in interest. Every extra year lowers the payment and raises the total."; `{n}` is the term typed. When the rounded-up payment clears it before the term (04 D-64, the engine's months actually paid), a `meta` line follows: "Paid off in month {m}, with a last payment of {x}." (D-45)); Annual rate row ("Annual rate" / "The rate on the quote", `%` pill input, **empty until typed**; a carried-in quick-check 0% shows "before any interest"); a toggle row "Secured against what you're buying" (defaults on for item type Vehicle, off otherwise; sets the debt's `secured` field).
   - **Instalment:** two tiles "Payment" and "How many" (1..600); note "No rate was quoted. {n} × {pay} is {total} against a {price} price, about {pct}% a year." (shown only when both numbers exist and the plan resolved; otherwise the plan is `missing` with "The payment and how many are needed to score this."). With on-the-day fees the fees are paid on the day ("On the day" = first instalment plus the fees) and the instalments cover the rest, so the note reads "No rate was quoted. {n} × {pay} is {total} against the {financed} paid over time, about {pct}% a year." When the instalments add up to less than they must cover, the plan is `missing` (flag "Below the price") and the note reads "{n} × {pay} is {total}, less than the {financed} it has to cover. Change the payment or how many to score this." (D-49, 04 ME-02 / LO-05).
   - **Card:** an eligibility card (neutral `fill1`; soft `Business` tint when capped; `dangerTint3` when blocked) with a status dot, title and body: ok "A card is accepted here" / "Nothing about this purchase blocks a card. What matters is how fast it comes back off."; capped "Only part of this can go on a card" / "A dealer card cap of {cap} applies. The other {rest} is counted as cash on the day."; blocked "This cannot go on a card" / "Rent, bills, loan repayments, tax and premiums are not card payments. A cash advance carries interest from the first day." with the action "Use deposit and loan instead". Then "How it gets cleared" chips ("In full next statement", "Over a few months", "Minimum only"); a months stepper (28px circles become 44 hit "−" / "+", figure "{n} mo"); under the card's rate row, the **"Minimum payment" row** (D-40, see "Card minimum payment" under Depth surfaces) that opens the card terms sheet; an optional money field **"Card fees (optional)"** (blank, never pre-filled; D-44, see "Card costs" under Depth surfaces); three tiles "Cleared" / "Interest" / "Card costs", where "Cleared" reads the months actually paid ("{n} mo", which can be fewer than the months set on the stepper, D-45) and "Card costs" sums the user's own card terms (the interest at the rate they typed plus the card fees they typed; engine `CardPlanDetail.cardCosts`, 04 D-63); a note. Never-clears (D-17): tile "Cleared" reads "Never", "Interest" and "Card costs" read "Endless"; open at the 600-month cap all three read "Not within 50 years" (a separate outcome, never inferred) and a `meta` line under the tiles states what is left: "Still owing {x} after 50 years at the minimum." (engine `remainingAtCap`; no cleared total or interest is shown for it, D-46). Closing optional card notes: three neutral questions kept with the decision (cover if income stops; no new spending on the card while it clears; would these payments still be made after a job loss) with chip answers; **these carry no warning, no colour and no verdict effect** (researcher assumption), heading "Your notes on this card", count "{n} of 3 answered".
   - **Investment term:** term chips and a note "{amt} a month for {n} months is {total} put in." (at 0% that is the whole figure; with a rate entered "At {r}% it would be worth about {fv}." followed by the caption "A projection at the rate you entered, not a forecast." in `meta` `inkFaint`).
5. **Not-a-lender footer** (`meta`, `inkFaint`, once at the bottom of the method area): "FincWin records and plans. It does not lend money, arrange credit or hold your funds." The card path shows no lender wording anywhere (no offers, approvals, "apply", pre-qualification or representative APR).
6. **Costs that follow it card:** header "Costs that follow it" (investment "Costs of holding it") with "a month" caption, explainer ("What owning it costs every month, less what it ends. The net figure is what the check uses." / "Platform and fund fees, if any. They come off the return, not your month."), `CheckRow`s including the read-only "{name} ends" rows from step 1 (greyed, "Confirmed on the item step"), "+ Add a running cost" (investment "+ Add a fee"), and the rule "Net monthly change" with the figure (`sheetTitle`) and math line "{gross} added, {repl} ends".

---

## Step 3: Impact (DCU-11)

The order is fixed: **verdict card, notices, warnings, then collapsible sections.**

### Verdict card

`ink` fill (`danger` fill for state `no`), `radii.card`, padding `space.cardPadLg` (20) on all sides, no shadow.

- Row 1: `StateChip` (left), then the optional "On the edge" tag (D-25); basis marker (right, `eyebrow` `inkSoft1`, right-aligned). **Wrap rule at default text size (card inner width 330):** the state chip (about 105) and the tag (about 75) stay together on line 1; the basis marker (about 150) shares line 1 only when no tag shows. With the tag, the basis marker drops to its own right-aligned line (+16 line, +4 gap = **+20 height**). The seed never shows the tag, so the frames keep the 259 budget; with the tag the verdict card is <= 279 and ends at or above y = 435, the two warning cards at or above y = 723 (still above the bar at 735). With the tag **and** the basis detail row together the second warning may end up to 13 below the bar and scroll; that combination is outside the DCU-11 frames.
- Row 2 (12 below, 27 high): **size label** as eyebrow text at left (`eyebrow`, `inkSoft1`, upper-cased): "Out of this month's spare" / "Out of savings" / "Needs spreading" (D-22, shown for every method); the **confidence chip** at right ("Strong basis" / "Fair basis" / "Rough basis", D-28, tappable).
- Title (10 below): `heading` role, `surface`, capped 1.3x, **at most 3 lines at default text size (copy about 64 characters or fewer)**. **One recorded exception:** the `inv` title for "saving and investing" ("The month closes only if {x} a month of saving and investing pauses.") is 65 characters before its amount, so it is capped at **72 characters** with demo-scale amounts; the string is locked and is checked on device at 402 wide (it must still fit 3 lines and keep the two warning cards above the bar). The `inv` `{x}` is a monthly pause amount, not a payment, so it is shown in **whole units** (as for totals and balances, assumption 12; D-54's cents apply to payments only), which keeps the 72-character exception safe. **Fallback if it wraps to 4 lines on device:** use "saving and investing" → "saving, investing" before any other change. If it still wraps, the title is clamped at 3 lines with the full text in the accessibility label.
- Body (10 below): `s13_5`, `surface` (on `ink`) with `inkSoft2` for the secondary sentence, **at most 2 lines at default text size**. **Normal sentence is shortened to fit one line with real amounts:** "{in} in, {out} out once this runs." (about 37 characters; the average/worst month is already in the basis marker). **Clamp: 2 lines with an ellipsis; the full text is in the accessibility label.** Line 1 is the difference line when one exists (priority: "Fits on its own; not with your other 1 open check" for D-24, else "Fits now; from {Month} the month is {x} short." for D-27), otherwise the normal sentence on one line; when the difference line fits on one line the normal sentence follows on line 2, and when it needs two lines the normal sentence is dropped from the card (it remains in the accessibility label and the month-by-month section). The remaining basis facts (one-offs, conversion) go to the basis detail row below the card.
- No buttons in the card. The group is one accessibility element: "{chip}{, on the edge}. {size}. {confidence}. {title}. {body}. {basis}" with `accessibilityLiveRegion="polite"`. Under D-56 the card updates on every keystroke, but the announcement is **debounced**: the live-region text is refreshed only after the same ~150 ms settle, or immediately when the state chip changes, so screen readers never read intermediate verdicts while the user types.

**Basis marker copy (short, one line at 402):**

| Basis | Words |
|---|---|
| Logged, average | "Average month · {in} in" |
| Logged, worst | "Worst month · {in} in" |
| First read (DCU-10) | "First read · your figures" |
| Real history just replaced the rough figures (D-04) | "Now from your logged months" (shown while the check's last seen basis was the first read) |

**Title copy by state** (ported from prototype L4476-4489, minus any invented rate):

| State / code | Title |
|---|---|
| no, month short and already short | "Your {average|worst} month is already {x} short. This makes it {y}." |
| no, month short | "The month ends {y} short." |
| no, `cashAfter` < 0 | "This would empty your cash." |
| missing, `card_not_accepted` | "A credit card cannot settle this kind of payment at all." |
| missing, card capped | (not missing: evaluated, D-18) |
| missing, `card_apr_missing` | "No rate on this card yet, so months and minimum can’t be scored." |
| missing, `card_minimum_terms_missing` | "Card minimum terms aren’t on record, so this can’t be scored." |
| missing, `dealer_cap_missing` | "No dealer cap is on record, so the card part can’t be scored." |
| missing, loan with no APR | "No rate was entered, so the loan cannot be scored yet." |
| missing, instalment amount or count absent | "Instalment amount or count is missing, so this can’t be scored." |
| missing, `instalment_total_below_price` (D-49) | "Instalments total less than the price, so this can’t be scored." |
| missing, `projection_rate_out_of_range` (D-48; unreachable through the bounded rate fields, mapped for completeness) | "A rate entered is above 100% a year, so this can’t be scored." |
| missing, split adds up to nothing | "The split adds up to nothing, so your share can’t be worked out." |
| **Cap (D-55):** every missing-state title is at most 64 characters (the longest are 64), like every other title, so none clamps at 402; the catalogue test asserts it | |
| fits | "It clears every line, with {left} still spare at month end." |
| adjust, first warning `cash` | "Your cash cushion breaks by {x}." |
| adjust, `dsr` | "Debt payments would reach {pct} of money in." |
| adjust, `dsr` with no money in (ratio null, D-50) | "With no money coming in, any debt payment breaks your line." |
| adjust, `owe` | "Unsecured debt would pass a year's income." |
| adjust, `inv` (D-35 pause) | "The month closes only if {x} a month of {investing|saving|saving and investing} pauses." |
| adjust, `cash_dip` first (warnings are ordered by shortfall, so the dip can lead) | "Your cash dips {x} under your cushion before payday." |
| no, a hard `cash_dip` alone (cash stays at or above 0 on the day and the month closes, but the 90-day path goes below 0) | "Your cash would run out before money comes in." |
| Precedence for `no` | cash below 0 on the day, then the month, then a hard cash dip; for `adjust` the title follows the first warning, and every warning code has one |

**Body copy:** fits "{in} in, {out} out once this is running." (+ household share sentence only in household scope, "Your share of it is {pct}."); every other state shows one short sentence (at most 2 lines): "{in} in on your {average|worst} month; {out} out once this is running." The Decide-home sources note moves into the "Assumptions in play" section, not the verdict card.

### Notices and warnings (D-05, D-06)

- **Warnings** (`WarningCard`, up to six, **ordered by shortfall, largest first**; the sixth is `cash_dip`, specified under Depth surfaces): row of dot (8px), title (`s13_5`, 600), value at right (`meta`, 600), then the description (`meta`, 500, `inkDim`). Card radius 26, padding `space.cardPad` (16) on all sides, 1.5px border. Hard = codes `month`, `owe`; soft = `cash`, `dsr`, `inv`.

| Code | Title | Value | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `month` | "The month does not close" | "−{short}" | Already short: "Your {average|worst} month is already {x} short before this purchase. Adding {new} takes the gap to {short}. That gap is borrowed money covering ordinary bills." Otherwise: "Every month would need {short} more than comes in." (the prototype's "Nothing else on this screen matters until that is fixed" is removed.) |
| `cash` | "Cash cushion broken" | "−{short}" | "You protect {cushion} as a cushion. Paying {day} on the day leaves {cashAfter}." |
| `dsr` | "Debt takes a large share of your income" | "{pct}" (no money in: "None in") | "Your line is {line}. After this, {debt} of {in} a month goes to debt payments." No money in on the basis (D-50): "Your line is {line} of money in. None comes in on this basis, so {debt} a month of debt payments is over it." |
| `owe` | "Unsecured debt above a year's income" | "{amt}" | "{amt} of unsecured debt against {annual} of annual income. Secured loans are not counted here. Your line is {mult} times average monthly income." |
| `inv` (D-35, includes saving) | "Investing pauses to close the month" / "Saving pauses..." / "Saving and investing pause..." | "−{x}/mo" | **Amber, states the amount, never says "should":** "The month closes only if {x} a month of investing pauses. That would take investing from {a} to {b} a month." Add, when the result is below the floor: "That is below the {floor} a month you protect." |

- **Card reasons** (extra `WarningCard`s that the engine's reason codes drive, ahead of the five, hard unless noted): `card_not_accepted` "Not accepted on a card" / "Declined" (reuse the eligibility copy); day-one cash remainder (soft) "{rest} is counted as cash on the day" / "{rest}" / "The card covers {cap} of a {price} price. The rest is tested as cash on the day, like any other day-one cost."; minimum payments (hard) "Minimum payments do not clear it" / "Never clears" or "{n} years" / "At the minimum, interest matches what you pay, so the balance never reaches zero." or "It takes {n} months — {yrs} years — and {int} in interest on a {cap} balance."; open at the cap (hard) "Not cleared within 50 years" / "{x} left" / "At the minimum, {x} is still owed after 50 years on a {balance} balance." (engine `remainingAtCap`; D-46). The prototype's question-driven cards (income stops, no commitment, job-loss cover, "Your income does not support this") are **dropped**; the first two duplicate engine warnings and the rest are UI judgement. The count rule still holds: warnings never exceed six engine warnings (including `cash_dip`) plus card reasons.
- **Neutral fact (04 D-59):** when a cash dip exists without the purchase (`cash_tight_before_payday`), one `fill1` note card (no dot, no colour): "Without this purchase, your cash is lowest on {date}, at {amt}." (with other open checks stacked: "Without this purchase, and with your other open checks, your cash is lowest on {date}, at {amt}."). It is never a warning and never changes the chip.
- **Deferred interest notice (D-42):** when the deferred-interest toggle is on and the engine reports `deferred_interest_charged` (04 D-48), one `fill1` note card (no dot, no colour): title "Interest charged back", body "If the balance isn’t cleared when the {n}-month promotion ends, {amt} of interest is charged back." The verdict state is the engine's; the card states the figure only.
- **Neutral fact (D-31):** when the engine returns `debt_rate_exceeds_return` on an invest check, one `fill1` note card (no dot, no colour): title `s13_5` "Debt rate and return", body `meta` "Your highest unsecured debt charges {apr}% a year. The return used here is {r}%." It never changes the chip.

### Collapsible sections (D-05)

All sections (up to nine) are **collapsed by default**, each a white `radii.card` card whose header is one `button` (min 56px, `accessibilityState.expanded`): eyebrow title at left, one-line summary (`meta`, `inkMuted`) beneath, a chevron that rotates 90 degrees (no animation). Expanding reveals the content with `fw-rise` (`DURATION.entrance`); reduced motion shows it instantly. Expanded state is remembered while the flow is open. Order and summaries:

| # | Section (eyebrow title) | Summary line (collapsed) | Content (from the prototype, copy rewritten where marked) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The month, line by line | "Left at month end: {now} now, {after} after" | Note "Money in on your {average|worst} month, against everything that leaves."; three-column table Line / Now / After (`meta`/`body`, rows: Money in, Living expenses, Debt payments, Saving and investing, Running costs of this (investment: This contribution), Left at month end (strong, top border 1.5px `ink`)); closing tile (`accentTint1` background when the month closes, `dangerTint3` when short): short "The month would be {x} short. That gap comes out of the cushion or goes onto borrowing." / closes "The month still closes with {x} spare, on your average month." or "... and that is your worst month, not your average." (the prototype's "card at {worstRate}%" fallback is removed) |
| 2 | Where it lands | "Five ledgers" | Note "Five ledgers. A purchase never touches only one."; Ledger / Now / After table for Income, Expenses, Debt, Cash, Investments with a 6px dot (`accent` better, `danger` worse, `inkSoft1` same) and the prototype descriptions |
| 3 | Day by day, next 90 days | "Lowest with this: {amt} on {date}" (or "Add your pay as a recurring series") | The 90-day `CashPathChart`, its text alternative and the unavailable state (see Depth surfaces) |
| 4 | Who pays what | "{n} people" | **Household only; absent until Phase 8.** Per-member avatar circle (member colour), name, role and share, day-one and monthly amounts |
| 5 | Against your lines | "{n} clear · {m} over" | Rows with a 8px dot (`accent` pass / `danger` fail) and value "Clear" / "Short {x}" / "Over" / "Squeezed": Cash cushion, Month closes, Debt service, Unsecured debt, Investing floor, Saving floor, each with the user's own line in the description (e.g. "{cushion} protected · {cashAfter} left on the day"; Debt service "{pct} against your {line} line", or with no money in "No money in on this basis, against your {line} line" and the value "Over", D-50). **Cushion row reads "{n} months, {reason}" (D-25); rows within 5% of their line carry an "On the edge" tag with "holds by {x}" / "misses by {x}"; the cash-path minimum is one of the rows** |
| 6 | With the expected income | "With the expected {amt} a month ({likely|uncertain}): {state}, pays for itself in {n} months" | **Only when a raise is set (step 1).** The separate card of D-32; the summary line is the whole card when collapsed. Never changes the verdict |
| 7 | If something goes wrong | "Runway {n} months, {m} less because of this" | Note "Switching one on shows it hitting your cash with this purchase already running."; four setback rows (checkbox, name, description, "−{hit}" and "{after} left" or "wiped out", "x"); "+ Add a setback to test"; when any is on, a result tile: "With those running, cash runs out. Cash left:" / "That takes you through the cushion you set. Cash left:" / "The cushion absorbs it. Cash left:" + figure. Defaults: Unexpected expense, Income drops, Job or client loss, Invoices pay late. **Adds "Runway lost to this purchase: {m} months" and a per-setback tag "New payments still met" / "New payments cannot be met" (D-32)** |
| 8 | What would make it work | "{n} options" | **Dark** card (`ink`): lead "In order of how much they move. The first two change the purchase; the rest change your month." (if the month closes: "Each option rescores the figures above immediately."); option rows (`surface`@.12 fill, radius 16, padding `space.gapMd` / `space.cardPad`): name (`label`, 600), value (`meta`, `accentTint1` or `dangerTint2`), description (`meta`, `inkSoft1`), optional action pill. Only present when the state is not "fits". Names are noun phrases, not instructions: "At {price} instead" ("Use this price"; for an instalment plan it also rescales the instalment amount exactly as the engine did when it found the price, D-49); "A {n}-month term" (value "+{amt}/mo" or "still short", "Use this term"); "A fifth off {line}" ("Open in Activity"); "{amt} more a month coming in"; "Saving first, then buying" ("Switch to wait"); "Without it" (value "{pay}/mo kept", description "Kept instead: {fv} over {n} months.", or "Kept instead: too large to project over {n} months." when the engine marks the figure out of range, D-47). Bisection runs exactly, so "At {price} instead" is the exact largest price that clears |
| 9 | Assumptions in play | "{n} assumptions" | `fill1` card; rows (name, source, value): Money in (months logged · steadiness · Average/Worst month), Expenses, Debt (n accounts · unsecured amount), **Return on money kept (0% unless you entered one)**, Running costs (n of m counted, net of k ended), plus the not-a-lender line. **Also: the sources note (months logged, expense lines, debts, accounts), cushion months and reason, one row per converted currency (rate, date) plus "Past lines keep the rate they were stamped with" when any foreign line exists (D-39), "Other open checks" rows ("{n} open check not counted: no price yet" / "... its amounts are in another currency"), "Card payments" · "Card payments aren’t counted until the minimum is set." while a card owes a balance with no minimum (D-40), "Card interest" · "Card minimums are counted without interest until the card’s rate is set." when a minimum includes interest but the card has no rate, "Loan payments" · "{n} loan balance has no repeating payment on it. Its payment counts only if it is among your bills." when a loan account owes a balance with neither a debt record nor a repeating series on it (D-38 minimal reading, DCU-24), and any basis facts that overflowed the basis detail row** |

Sections 4 (Who pays what, household only), 6 (With the expected income, only with a raise) and 8 (What would make it work, absent when the state is "fits") are omitted entirely when not applicable (no empty frames); section 3 never omits and shows its unavailable state.
