## Step 4: Alternatives (DCU-05, DCU-06, DCU-11)

**Order (deviation from the prototype, for the screenshot budget):** the "Four ways" table first, then "The cheaper one", then the trade line. The alternatives table is free, with no lock chrome (D-17).

1. **"Four ways, same {n} months" card:** eyebrow title and **one** sub-line (`meta`): "Kept at 0% unless you add a rate" (with the "+ Add a rate" link, see below) or "Kept, at {r}% a year". The longer explanation moves to the footer. `{n}` is the engine's alternatives horizon, which follows the months the plan is actually paid (D-45), not the term typed.
   - **Four stacked `OptionRow`s** (the 4-column grid of the prototype is replaced because the 134px name column cannot hold a name, descriptor and reason at 402 wide): each row, padding `space.rowPadDense` (13) vertical, 1px `fill2` separators, "As planned" row tinted `fill5`, **row <= 114**:
     - name (`sheetTitle`) — "As planned", "Cheaper one", "Wait and save", "Not buying it";
     - descriptor (`meta`, `inkFaint`, **one line at default text size**, up to 2 lines only above 1.3x text): as planned "{price} · {method}"; cheaper "{price} · deposit scaled to match" for a loan, "{price} · instalments scaled to match" for an instalment plan (the engine scales the instalments with the price, 04 HI-03), otherwise "{price} · {method}" (D-49); wait "Bought at month {n}, then {pay} a month kept"; skip "You own nothing";
     - a three-column `FigureStrip`: **On the day**, **A month**, **Kept** (header "Kept, at {r}%" when the user has entered a rate, otherwise "Kept"); figures `body`; column captions `tabLabel`.
   - **Suppression (DCU-06):** when the engine suppresses a row, **Kept shows "—"** and **one reason line** (`meta`, `warn1`, no pill chrome so the row stays compact, one line, +22) sits under the strip. Reason lines count toward the fold budget (at most three, since As planned never has one). Reason copy (each <= 44 characters, from the engine's suppression codes): "Month still {x} short on this plan"; "Only {x} a month left — too thin to count"; "Nothing freed against buying as planned"; "Takes {n} months — past the {m}-month horizon"; "Month is {x} short — nothing spare"; "No cash above cushion, no monthly surplus". Money that is not genuinely spare is never shown as a kept figure. **Out of range (D-47):** when the engine reports a kept figure as too large to compute exactly (`exceeds_range`, a high rate over a long horizon), Kept shows "—" and the one reason line "Too large to project at this rate" (33 characters); a row is never both suppressed and out of range, so it still has at most one reason line and the fold budget holds.
   - The as-planned row's Kept is "—" with no reason line; the footer carries "Nothing is freed to save on the planned route."
   - Footer (`meta`, `inkFaint`, below the fold in the DCU-11 frame): "The last figure is money kept: the cash you keep on the day plus the payment you never make, capped at what is genuinely spare — {spareCash} above your cushion and {spareMonth} a month. Each row is measured against buying as planned — money set aside, plus growth only if you add a rate, no tax or fees, and no guarantee." plus the conditional sentences of the prototype (cheaper row starts with the day-one saving; waiting buys outright at month {n} and then keeps the {pay} a month you no longer owe).
2. **"Cheaper, and what it leaves" card** (title and sub-copy amended 2026-10-22, WR-D04; title shortened 2026-10-23 by user decision so the eyebrow fits the 32-character cap of rule E, no exception; sub-copy "The difference stays yours for the whole {n} months."): figure "Cheaper one" (`heading`) and "Not spent" (`sheetTitle`, `accent`) with the note ("{x} less on the day, then {y} a month" / "All of it on the day" / "{y} a month, nothing on the day" / "Nothing changes on this plan"); `StepSlider` 30%..100% step 1 (aria "Cheaper price", value text "{pct} percent of {price}"); note "{pct}% of {price}". **Return control:** a text link "+ Add a rate" (`accent`, 44 hit); tapping reveals a `%` pill input that accepts up to 100% a year (the engine's bound; a higher value is refused and the field's hint reads "Up to 100% a year", D-48); once set, a chip "{r}% a year" with an "x" that returns to 0%. Default is 0%, with no pre-filled rate and no "keeps earning" wording (D-07, 04 D-06).
3. **"The trade, in one line" card** (`ink`): eyebrow, line (`sheetTitle`, `surface`) "The trade appears as the slider moves." / "Buying at {cheaper} lowers the payment from {a} to {b}, but the month still only leaves {left} — nothing you could reliably set aside." / "Buying at {cheaper} leaves {today} today and {y} a month kept. Kept up until month {n}, that is {fv} — {own} of your own money{ and {growth} it earned, only when a rate is set}." Two buttons: "Use the cheaper price" (`surface` fill, `ink` text; it sets the price, and for a loan the deposit and for an instalment plan the instalment amount, exactly as the cheaper row computed them, so the rescored check is the row the user saw, D-49) and "Wait and save" (`surface`@.12, `surface` text) which jumps to step 5 with Wait chosen.

---

## Step 5: The decision (DCU-07, D-09, D-10, D-11, D-12)

1. **Plan card** ("The plan on the table"): plan line (`sheetTitle`) "{price} · {method}" and a `FigureStrip` "On the day" / "A month" / "Left after" (the last in `danger` when negative).
2. **Outcome `ChoiceCard`s** (radio group, 3): **Go ahead** — description "Writes the expense, the schedule and the running costs into your records. Every payment appears in Activity against this check." (if the month is short: "Logs it anyway. The record will show the month was {x} short when you signed."); flag chip when state is not "fits": "Breaks a line" / "Against your lines" (amber/`dangerTint3` pill; on the selected dark card the flag uses `surface`@.12 fill with `dangerTint2` text). **Wait and save** — "Sets an amount and a date and starts a savings goal for this plan. A reminder opens this check on that day, recalculated. Nothing in your balances moves until then." **Pass** — "Kept in Past decisions until you delete it. No reminders, and it can be reopened as a new check. Not spending is not saving, so no balance changes." (replaces the prototype's "filed for twelve months").
3. **Wait panel** (only when Wait is selected; adds the goal progress bar preview "0 of {target} saved", see Depth surfaces): "Set aside each month" figure (`heading`), "Ready by" date (`sheetTitle`, `accent`), `StepSlider` step 25 (min 25, max twice what is left each month, aria "Monthly saving"), note "To cover {target} without touching your {cushion} cushion. Your month currently leaves {left}, so {amt} is {more than you actually have spare — something else has to give | within what you have spare}." Ready-by is derived, not picked (no date picker).
3b. **Cooling-off toggle** (only for `needs_spreading`): "Check again in 48 hours", off by default (Depth surfaces).
4. **"What gets written" card** (`fill1`, radius 26, padding `space.cardPadLg`): heading "Nothing is written yet" (no outcome) / "What gets written" (outcome chosen), bulleted lines (6px `accent` dot, `s13_5`, `inkDim`). Lines by outcome:
   - **Go ahead (D-09):** cash "A pending expense of {amt} on {date}, in {account}."; loan "A debt of {amt}{, secured} at {apr}%, and a recurring payment of {pay} a month for {n} months.", then "Then {pay} a month for {n} months." for each later run of payments (a promotion's second rate) and "A last payment of {pay} in month {n}." for a smaller closing payment (the lines follow the engine's payment runs, which is exactly what Go ahead writes; `{n}` counts the months actually paid, D-45); instalment "A pending expense of {amt} on {date} for the day-one cost, in {account}." (the first instalment plus any on-the-day fees, 04 ME-02) then "A recurring payment of {pay} a month, {n} times." ({n} = the later instalments, as Go ahead writes them, D-49); card "A pending expense of {amt} on {card}."; running costs "{n} running costs as recurring monthly lines, net {+/−}{amt} a month, and {m} existing expenses closed."; household "Split across {n} people, your share {pct}." (hidden until Phase 8); then "Your saving and investing continue untouched." or "Your investing drops to {amt} a month while this runs."
   - **Wait:** "A savings goal of {target}, {amt} a month, ready {date}." / "A reminder on {date} that opens this check, recalculated." / "Nothing else changes: no balance or limit moves until you reopen it." (D-34: Wait creates the savings goal and nothing else) / "The quote and every assumption are kept exactly as they are now."
   - **Pass:** "Kept in Past decisions with the price and your reason." / "Nothing added to savings or net worth. Not spending is not saving." / "No reminders. It stays until you delete it."
   - **Any outcome, when "Check again in 48 hours" is on:** adds the line "A reminder in 48 hours that reopens this check, recalculated." (so Pass reads "No reminders" only when cooling-off is off; with it on, the Pass list shows "No other reminders.")

### Commit behaviour

- **Go ahead:** the primary button opens a `ConfirmSheet`: heading "Write this to your records?", body = the same "What gets written" list, actions "Go ahead" (primary) / "Not yet" (default; backdrop and Android back count as "Not yet"). Confirming writes everything in one step, fires `hapticMedium`, returns to Decide home and shows the toast "Written to your records" with Undo; when the verdict was not "fits" the toast reads "Logged. The record notes it breaks a line." Undo removes the whole set in one step (02.x undo stack). The check moves to Past decisions as "Bought".
- **Wait and save:** no confirm. It saves the decision and creates its savings goal row (target, monthly amount, ready-by date) with amount and ready-by date, schedules the local reminder, returns to Decide home, toast "Saved. Reminder set for {date}". If notifications are not permitted at that moment the OS permission prompt is requested first; if declined the decision still saves and the toast reads "Saved. Notifications are off, so no reminder will arrive." The reminder text (no amounts, no item name, since it shows on a lock screen): title "Check ready", body "A plan you saved has reached its ready-by date. Open it to see where it stands now." Tapping it reopens the check at step 3, recomputed.
- **Pass:** no confirm, no haptic beyond selection. Goes to Past decisions with the toast "Passed. Nothing moved."
- **No review prompt is ever requested** after "Doesn’t fit right now", after any `no`, or in the first session (LAUNCH §4). A prompt after an estimate-vs-actual that lands at or under the estimate belongs to a later phase.

---

## Past decisions (DCU-07, D-11, D-12)

**List screen** (pushed from the Past decisions row): detail header (38px back circle, title "Past decisions", `sheetTitle`), sub-copy "What you decided, and what it actually cost once it was running." One white `radii.card` list. Row (padding 15/18; a Waiting row also shows the goal progress bar and "{saved} of {target} saved" under the sub-line): name (`body`) + chevron, `StateChip` ("Bought" / "Waiting" / "Passed"), optional "Reopen" pill (`fill1`, 44 high; on Waiting and Passed only, creating a new check), sub-line (`meta`, `inkFaint`: Bought "{price} · {method} · {date}"; Waiting "{target} target · {amt} a month · ready {date}"; Passed "{price} quoted · {why} · {date}"), and for Bought a **delta strip** (`fill6`, radius 16, padding `space.gapSm` / `space.gapMd`): left `meta` "Estimated {est} a month to run, actually" and right figure (`label`, `danger` if over, `accent` if at or under) "{actual}", or for a payment plan "Payments matched the schedule for" "{n} of {m}". Empty state: `EmptyState` "Nothing decided yet" / "Checks you act on, save for or pass on are kept here." Rows sort newest first.

**Decision detail sheet** (tap a row): title = name, chip at right; a white tile of label/value lines (`meta` label `inkDim`, value `label`): Final price; How it was paid; Estimated on the day vs Actual on the day; Estimated a month vs Actual a month; Reviewed ("Compared at 30 and 90 days"); **Linked lines ({n})**. A note paragraph (`meta`, `inkDim`). Actions: "Remove" (`dangerTint2` fill, `danger` text, 44+ high), "Reopen the check" where allowed (accent), "Close".

**Estimate-vs-actual (D-12):** compares the estimate with the **sum of the linked lines** (the lines Go ahead wrote plus any the user attaches) at **30 days and 90 days** after the decision, for the **day-one cost** and the **monthly cost** (payment plus running costs), numbers only, no new entry.
- Before day 30: the lines read "Compared at 30 days — {date}" and no delta shows.
- At 30 and 90 days: "{label} at 30 days: {actual} against {estimate} ({+/−}{diff})". Over the estimate: diff in `danger` with a plus sign and the word "over"; at or under: `accent` with "at" or "under". Words always accompany colour.
- Attach: a "Attach a line" `Pill` (secondary) opens the `AttachLineSheet` row picker; each linked line shows name, date, amount and a 44px "x" that unlinks it (undo toast "Line unlinked"). **What a line counts as is set by its date, with no picker** (OQ-A9): a line dated within 7 days of the decision counts toward the day-one cost, later lines toward the monthly cost; lines Go ahead wrote keep the role they were written with. The note carries the prototype's neutral reading: "Running {x} a month over the estimate." without the prototype's "worth confirming those by hand" (imperative).
- **Remove:** `ConfirmSheet` heading "Remove this decision?", body "It leaves Past decisions. Lines it wrote stay in Activity.", actions "Keep it" / "Remove" (`danger`). Removing a decision never deletes the lines it wrote.
- Pass detail lines: Quoted; Why it was passed; Decided {date}; and **no** "Kept on file until" line (replaced by D-11: "Kept until you delete it").

---

## Depth surfaces (D-24..D-37; engine 04 D-41..D-58)

Every surface below is specified at its component level and given an explicit **placement** so the DCU-11 fold budgets above stay intact. Rule: nothing in this section adds height above the step 3 verdict card, the two warning cards, or the four step 4 option rows, except the single optional one-line "basis detail" row already budgeted (see Budget). Chart and chip components use §2 tokens only and `react-native-svg`; no gradients, no extra shadows, no new colour.

### Placement summary

| Surface | Decision | Lives in | Above the fold? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Combined-checks toggle | D-24 | Flow header right slot (all steps) and the quick-check footer row; shown only with 2 or more open checks | Header slot sits inside the existing header row, so no added height. Quick check: +26 on the footer row, result still above the fold |
| Standalone-vs-combined line | D-24 | Step 3 verdict body, line 1 | Inside the existing 2-line body cap |
| Cushion months and reason | D-25 | "Against your lines" section row; "Cash never below" limit row and its editor | Below the fold (collapsed section / Decide home lower cards) |
| "On the edge" tag | D-25 | Verdict card row 1, beside the state chip; per-row tags in "Against your lines" | Row 1 (no added height) |
| Cash-path chart and `cash_dip` card | D-26 | New collapsible section "Day by day, next 90 days" (section 3); the `cash_dip` card is an ordinary warning | Chart collapsed, below the fold. The `cash_dip` card is one of the ordered warnings and uses the 132 budget |
| Changes coming up | D-27 | Decide home card between Open checks and Past decisions, plus an add/edit sheet | Below the fold on Decide home |
| Worst-month line ("Fits now; from June...") | D-27 | Verdict body line 1 when no combined difference exists; else in the basis detail row | Inside the body cap / the one optional row |
| Confidence chip and reasons sheet | D-28 | Verdict card row 2, right | Row 2 height becomes 27 (budgeted) |
| One-off toggle; "1 one-off left out" | D-29 | Record line detail and entry sheet; basis detail row on step 3 | Basis detail row (optional, budgeted) |
| Promo rate (with the deferred-interest toggle, D-42), variable-rate stress, BNPL late fee | D-30, D-42 | Step 2 method panels; the deferred-interest notice is a step 3 note card | Step 2 has no fold budget; the demo seed has no promotion, so the notice never shows in frames 1-3 |
| Card minimum payment | D-40 | Step 2 card panel row and the card terms sheet; "Minimum payment not set" in the basis detail row and Assumptions | Basis detail row (optional, budgeted); the demo seed's cards carry terms, so it never shows in frames 1-3 |
| Investing-instead section (with the holding fee, D-44) | D-31, D-44 | Step 4, collapsible section after "The trade, in one line" | Below the fold |
| Income-change input | D-32 | Step 1, card after "When and why" | Step 1 has no fold budget |
| "With expected income" card, payback | D-32 | Step 3 collapsible section 6 (summary line reads as the card) | Collapsed, below the fold |
| Setbacks runway lost | D-32 | Step 3 section "If something goes wrong" | Collapsed, below the fold |
| Running-cost prompts (with the loss-of-value entry, D-44) | D-33, D-44 | Step 2 "Costs that follow it" card | Step 2 |
| Card fees field and "Card costs" tile | D-44 | Step 2 card panel | Step 2 has no fold budget |
| Savings goal progress bar | D-34 | Step 5 Wait panel (preview at 0), the decided check's step 5 (a Waiting check), the Past decisions row, the decision detail sheet. Not on Open checks: a Waiting check has left that list | Step 5 and lists |
| Cooling-off toggle | D-35 | Step 5, between the outcome cards and "What gets written", only for `needs_spreading` | Step 5 |
| Estimate-gap line | D-36 | Step 2, under "Costs that follow it" heading and under the payment cards | Step 2 |
| Currency conversion basis line; missing-rate state | D-37 | Step 3 basis detail row; verdict card when `missing` | Basis detail row / verdict card |

### Basis detail row (step 3, optional, 25px)

One `meta` line (`inkMuted`, wraps at large text, absent when empty) directly under the verdict card, 8 below it, items joined by " · " in this priority: the worst-month line (if it was not shown in the body), "{n} one-off left out" / "{n} one-offs left out" (D-29), the conversion basis "Includes amounts converted from {CUR} at {rate} on {date}" (D-37; several currencies: "Includes amounts converted from {CUR}, {CUR} at the latest stored rates, {date}"), "Cash path unavailable" (`cash_path_unavailable`), "Card payments not counted until the minimum is set" (D-40, a card owing a balance with no minimum; on an open check the row is then a 44-high button, hint "Opens the card minimum payment", that opens the card terms sheet for that card). Overflow goes into the "Assumptions in play" rows, never a second line. **The demo seed has none of these** (no one-offs, no foreign-currency lines, pay series present, no future changes), so frames 1-3 never show the row; if present, it adds 25 and the budget still holds (703 + 25 = 728, above the bar at 735).

### Combined checks (D-24)

- **Toggle:** appears only when 2 or more checks are open. Header slot: a pill button, visual 32px high / hit 44px, `fill1` fill, label `tabLabel` "With other checks" (accessible name "With my other open checks"), a 10px state dot (`accent` on, `line4` off) plus `accessibilityState.checked`; role `switch`. **On by default**, remembered per check. `selectionAsync` haptic. It recomputes the verdict, warnings, alternatives and quick-check result immediately.
- **With exactly one open check** (OQ-A3 declined, D-43): the pill stays hidden on both surfaces; the check is still judged with that one open check (05 D-24 amended), and the quick-check footer reads "Worked out with your other open check." so the combined basis is stated.
- **Quick-check placement:** the footer row ("Worked out from the figures you entered.") gains the same pill at the right; the row becomes 44px high (+26). Credit tallest state: chip, size label, line and monthly figure still end at or above y = 543, footer row ends at or above y = 594, buttons stay below the fold as before.
- **Standalone-vs-combined line:** when standalone and combined states differ, verdict body line 1 reads "Fits on its own; not with your other 1 open check" ("... {n} open checks"); the second body line is the normal short sentence. The state chip, title and warnings are for the combined position. When they match, no line shows.
- When the toggle is off, the verdict is standalone and the body shows only the normal sentence.
- **Seed constraint (amended 2026-10-15, 05 D-24 amended):** every open check combines with all the others. Frame 1 is captured on the sofa check with "With my other open checks" switched off (a real, per-check user setting), so it shows "It fits" standalone. Frames 2-3 use the car's combined position, which must stay "Needs adjustment". Cushion copy under 3 full months: "3 months — your income steadiness isn't known yet". With a cash path, bills due soon show only as `cash_dip`; the cushion card covers the day-one cost alone.

### Cushion months and "on the edge" (D-25)

- **Cushion line (Against your lines, row "Cash cushion"):** description "{cushion} protected · {cashAfter} left on the day · {n} months, {reason}". Reasons: "because your income is steady" (3), "because your income varies" (4.5), "because your income is volatile" (6). **Under 3 full months the row reads "3 months — your income steadiness isn’t known yet"** (05-CONTEXT amended, 04 D-42 amended) and the confidence chip shows "Rough basis". A cushion the user typed reads "your own line".
- **Lines card (Decide home):** the "Cash never below" default tag becomes "default · {n} months"; the editor's info line adds "The default is {n} months of your costs, {reason}."
- **Edge tag:** a `tabLabel` pill (`categoryTint.Business` / `warn1`) reading "On the edge" sits beside the state chip in verdict row 1, shown when any line has `on_edge`. The margin text lives in the rows of "Against your lines" (value column adds "holds by {x}" / "misses by {x}" in `warn1` under "Clear"/"Short"), and in the accessibility label of the chip ("It fits, on the edge: the cushion holds by {x}"). The verdict state is unchanged.

### Day by day, next 90 days (D-26)

- **Section 3 of the step 3 sections**, collapsed by default. Summary: "Lowest with this: {amt} on {date}".
- **Chart (`CashPathChart`, `react-native-svg`):** width = card inner width (about 330), height 148; padding left 36 / right 8 / top 8 / bottom 22; 90 daily points.
  - Two paths: **with the purchase** solid `ink` 2px; **without** dashed (4 on, 4 off) `inkSoft1` 2px. Never colour alone: the legend names both ("With this", "Without"), dash vs solid is the second cue.
  - Zero line `line3` 1px with "0" label; if a cushion exists, a 1px dashed `warn1` line labelled "Cushion".
  - Lowest day with the purchase marked by a 6px circle (`danger` when below 0, `warn1` when below the cushion, `ink` otherwise) with a `tabLabel` readout "{amt} · {date}". Lowest without is marked with a hollow 5px circle.
  - Axis labels `tabLabel` `inkMuted`: y at max / 0 / min (rounded to whole units), x at day 0, 30, 60, 90 ("Today", "+30 d", "+60 d", "+90 d").
  - Interaction: a horizontal drag/press scrubs a vertical cursor and shows "{date}: {with} with, {without} without" in a readout above the chart (`meta`). Not required to read the chart; the text list below carries the same facts.
  - Text alternative (always shown below): "Lowest with this: {amt} on {date}. Lowest without: {amt} on {date}." The chart has `accessibilityRole="image"` and `accessibilityLabel` equal to that sentence.
  - Reduced motion: the chart draws once with no animation.
- **Unavailable (`cash_path_unavailable`):** the section stays, collapsed summary "Add your pay as a recurring series", expanded body "Add your pay as a recurring series to see day-by-day cash." plus a text link "Add a recurring series" (accent, 44 hit) that opens Record's series flow. The basis detail row reads "Cash path unavailable".
- **`cash_dip` warning card** (ordered with the others by shortfall; counts toward the six; fires only when the purchase causes or deepens the dip, 04 D-59): hard (`danger` palette) when the lowest balance with the purchase is below 0, soft (amber) when below the cushion. Both tones: title "Cash runs low before money comes in", value "−{extra}", description "This purchase takes your lowest point before payday {extra} lower: {lowest} on {date}." `{extra}` is only the extra depth the purchase adds (the engine's shortfall), never the whole gap; the date and lowest amount are the with-purchase low. A dip that exists without the purchase is the neutral fact card under Notices, never this card.

### Changes coming up (D-27)

- **Card on Decide home** (order: after Open checks, before Past decisions). Heading row: `label` "Changes coming up" at left, "+ Add a change" (`label`, `accent`, 44 hit) at right. White `radii.card` list; rows (padding `rowPad`): name (`body`) with a `StateChip`-style tag "Income" (accent tint) or "Spending" (`fill1` / `inkDim`) at the right; sub-line (`meta`, `inkFaint`): "{+/−}{amt} a month · from {Mon YYYY}" and " · until {Mon YYYY}" when ended; a 44px "x" removes (undo toast "Change removed"). Tap edits.
- **Empty:** "Nothing dated yet" / "A raise, a rent change or a bill ending: add it and every check looks 12 months ahead."
- **Add/edit sheet:** title "Add a change" / "Edit change"; fields: segmented "Income" | "Spending"; "Goes up" | "Goes down"; Name (optional, placeholder "What changes"); "Amount a month" (money); "Starts" (`DateField`, `minDate` = first day of this month, shows month and year); "Ends" (`DateField` plus a toggle "No end date", on by default). Buttons "Cancel" / "Save change". Dirty-discard per 03 D-26. Stored per user.
- **Effect on the verdict:** the month ledger uses the worst of the next 12 months. When that month differs from today's, the verdict body line 1 reads "Fits now; from {Month} the month is {x} short." A raise never cancels an earlier shortfall.

### Confidence chip and reasons sheet (D-28)

- Verdict card row 2, right-aligned, `tabLabel` chip with a text label: "Strong basis" (`accent`@.12 over `fill1`, `accent` text), "Fair basis" (`fill1` / `inkDim`), "Rough basis" (`categoryTint.Business` / `warn1`). It is a button (44 hit) with the name "Basis: {strength}, tap for reasons".
- **Reasons sheet** (`DetailSheet`, title "What this rests on"): one row per engine reason code, e.g. "{n} full months logged", "Income is steady / varies / is volatile", "This is a first read from the figures you gave", "{n} one-off left out", "Includes amounts converted from {CUR}". Footer `meta`: "Strong needs 6 full months with steady income; fair needs 3; anything less is rough." Button "Close".

### One-off lines in Record (D-29)

- Record's line detail / edit form gains a `ToggleRow` **"One-off, won't recur"** (off by default), sub-copy "Left out of the averages Decide uses." Placed under Category in the entry sheet and in `TransactionDetailSheet` edit mode; it saves with the row. The field travels in the snapshot. Record screens keep their own spec otherwise.
- Basis wording on step 3 (see basis detail row): "1 one-off left out" / "{n} one-offs left out". An unmarked spike still counts (no change to that rule).

### Credit terms in step 2 (D-30)

All three live in the method panels; the quick check (D-23) shows none of them. All inputs start empty; no value is ever pre-filled.
- **Promotional rate (Loan and Card panels):** a collapsed row "+ Promotional rate". Expanded: "Promotional rate" (`%`), "For" (months), "Then" (`%`, the rate after). Summary once filled: "{r}% for {n} months, then {r2}%". Results tiles: first "To clear it inside the promotion: {x} a month" (D-53, user 2026-10-16: the engine's `paymentToClearInPromo`, rounded up, so paying it for the promotion's months clears the balance and no interest is charged back; shown only when a promotion is entered and scored; a figure, never an instruction); "Payment after the promotion: {x} a month"; "The balance clears before it ends" or "Left when it ends: {x}" (the balance after the promotion's last month, read from the same payment schedule the engine uses, D-52); "Interest after it ends: {x}"; and a `meta` line "The month test uses the highest payment, {x}."
  - **Deferred-interest toggle (D-42):** a `ToggleRow` **"Interest is charged back if it isn’t cleared"**, sub-copy "Only on a 0% promotion. Off unless you turn it on.", shown inside the expanded promo row once a promotional rate of 0 is typed (the engine accepts deferred interest only on a 0% promotion, 04-05 `PromoTerms`); hidden, and sent as off, for any other rate. **Default off**; never inferred. When on and the balance would still be open at the promo end, the results add the tile "Charged back if it isn’t cleared by the end: {x}" and step 3 shows the deferred-interest notice card (04 D-48 `deferred_interest_charged`). Declarative; no "should". There is no deferred-interest Assumptions row.
- **Variable rate (Loan and Card):** a `ToggleRow` "The rate can change" (off by default). On: a stress card (`fill1`, radius 16) with two lines, each with a small state word chip: "At +2 points: {x} a month — {Fits|Needs adjustment|Not affordable}" and "At +4 points: {y} a month — {state}". Caption `meta`: "The verdict stays on the rate you entered."
- **Late fee (Instalment panel):** a money field "Late fee (optional)" and, once filled, `meta` lines "If 1 payment is late: +{x}" and "If 2 are late: +{y}". These never change the verdict.

### Card minimum payment (step 2 card panel, D-40, D-38)

- **Where:** the card panel of step 2, directly under the card's rate row: a `Row` "Minimum payment" with chevron, value text (`meta`) "{p}% of the balance plus interest, at least {floor}" / "{p}% of the balance, at least {floor}" / "Paid in full monthly" / **"Minimum payment not set"** (`warn1`). Tapping opens the **card terms sheet**. The terms live on the card's debt record (the `debts` row of kind card, D-38), so they apply to every check and to the month, not only this check.
- **Pre-fill:** a card with no stored terms shows the per-market terms (04 D-05/D-16; e.g. US 1% + interest, $25 floor, converted to the home currency) with the `meta` note "Pre-filled from the standard card terms for your region. Your card’s own terms can differ." For the Canada row the note reads "Pre-filled from the standard card terms outside Quebec. Your card’s own terms can differ." (the region is country-level, so Quebec users get the CA row; a province field for the CA-QC row is deferred). Nothing is pre-filled when the region has no market row; the row then reads "Minimum payment not set".
- **Card terms sheet** (`Sheet`, `SheetHeader` "Cancel" / "Save terms"; title "Card minimum payment"): "Percent of the balance" (`%` pill input), a `ToggleRow` "Plus that month’s interest", "At least" (money input), and a `ToggleRow` **"Paid in full monthly"** (sub-copy "No minimum is counted. The card’s spending is already in your month."); when it is on the three term fields are disabled. A text link "Use the standard terms" restores the market pre-fill (hidden with no market row). Dirty discard per 03 D-26. Saving writes the terms and the flag to the card's debt record; the rate row keeps saving the rate.
- **Effect:** the month counts the card's minimum on its balance (04 D-16 terms); "Paid in full monthly" adds no minimum (the card's spending lines already count once). A card owing a balance with no terms and no market row adds nothing, and step 3 notes it in the basis detail row ("Card payments not counted until the minimum is set") and in Assumptions ("Card payments aren’t counted until the minimum is set."). On an open check both step 3 notes are tappable (chevron in Assumptions, 44 hit, `accessibilityRole` button, hint "Opens the card minimum payment") and open this card terms sheet for the affected card (the first one when several owe without a minimum; after saving, the next becomes the target); a decided check shows them without the tap (D-40 "editable on the debt").

### Card costs (step 2 card panel, D-44, 04 D-63)

- **Card fees field:** under the "Minimum payment" row, a money pill input "Card fees (optional)" with `meta` sub-copy "Fees the card charges for this purchase. Paid on the day; other fees go on the price card." Blank by default and never pre-filled (04 D-07). A typed fee is paid on the day: it adds to "On the day" and "Total paid" on the card's `ChoiceCard` (engine `cardFees`). A fee typed here is not also asked for on the price card; the sub-copy points other fees there.
- **"Card costs" tile:** the engine's `CardPlanDetail.cardCosts`, the user's own card terms summed: interest at the card rate they typed (promotion and charged-back interest included) plus the card fees they typed. With a fee typed, a `meta` line under the tiles reads "{interest} interest plus {fees} in card fees." A minimum that never clears reads "Endless" (as the Interest tile); at the 600-month cap "Not within 50 years". This replaces the earlier `plan.total − plan.price` / "—" reading.

### Investing instead (step 4, D-31)

- **Placement:** a collapsible section after "The trade, in one line" (below the fold), collapsed by default, eyebrow title **"If it went into investing instead"**, summary "A projection at the rate you enter". It uses the shared `CollapsibleSection`. The section is Pro-candidate-free (`canUse` true).
- **Rates:** a chips row shows "0%" (fixed, always present, not removable) and up to three user rates as chips "{r}% a year" with a 44px "x"; "+ Add a rate" (`accent` link, opens a `%` pill input that accepts up to 100% a year, hint "Up to 100% a year" on a refused value, D-48) is hidden at three (hint "Up to 3 rates"). The app never suggests or pre-fills a rate. These are the check's single rate list (see assumption 17); the "Kept" column uses the first rate in it, or 0% if none.
- **Holding fee (D-44, 04 D-63):** under the rates row, a `%` pill input "Holding fee (optional)" with suffix "a year" and `meta` sub-copy "Platform and fund fees a year, if any. Nothing is assumed." Blank by default; nothing is ever pre-filled. Once typed, each projection grows at the entered rate less the fee (engine `netRates`; at 0% the held money shrinks by the fee). Each rate row label reads "{r}% a year, {net}% after fees" (the net can read below 0, with a minus sign), and the caption on every view becomes "A projection at the rate you entered less the {f}% fee, not a forecast." (0% row: "At 0% the money is only held, and the {f}% fee comes off it."). The fee applies to the four views only; the "Kept" column and the alternatives table are money kept as cash or savings and are never labelled "after fees".
- **Horizon:** `Chip` row "12", "24", "36", "60", "120" months plus the plan term if different; selected by default = the plan term (the months the plan is actually paid, which is the engine's default horizon, D-45), or 60 for cash; label "Over {n} months".
- **Four views**, each a `fill1`-bordered white inner tile (radius 16, padding 16) stacked, each with a heading (`label`), a figure table of rows per rate (`meta` rate label left, `body` figure right) and the caption under the figures, **on every view: "A projection at the rate you entered, not a forecast."** (`meta`, `inkFaint`; at 0% the caption reads "At 0% the money is only held, not grown.")
  1. "Price invested instead": "{share} put in once. After {n} months: {fv}."
  2. "Payments invested instead": "{pay} a month for {n} months, running costs included. After {n} months: {fv}."
  3. "Investing growth lost to a pause": shown only when the plan pauses investing: "{amt} a month paused. Growth not earned over {n} months: {fv}." Otherwise a single `meta` line "This plan pauses no investing."
  4. "Net worth, with and without this" (`NetWorthChart`, same construction as the cash path chart): a segmented control of the available rates ("0%", "{r}%"...) chooses which rate is drawn; two lines, **without** dashed `inkSoft1`, **with** solid `ink`; end labels with the final values; x axis in months at quarter points of the horizon; text alternative "After {n} months: {a} with this, {b} without, at {r}%." **Out of range (D-47):** a figure the engine marks as too large to compute exactly reads "Too large to project at this rate" in the view rows; the chart draws only the in-range part of a line (a gap is never drawn as 0), an end label whose final point is out of range reads the same words, and the text alternative becomes "After {n} months the path is too large to project at {r}%."; with no in-range point at all, only that sentence shows.
- Copy is declarative only. The section never uses "invest now" or any instruction. All figures are `useMoneyFormatter` output in the home currency.

### Income change (step 1) and "with expected income" (step 3) (D-32)

- **Step 1 card** ("Does this change your income?", after the "When and why" card): a `ToggleRow` "This changes my income" (off). On: segmented "Raises" | "Lowers"; "Amount a month" (money); "Starts" (`DateField`, `minDate` today); "How sure" segmented "Likely" | "Uncertain". Notes under it: Lowers "Counted in the verdict."; Raises "Not counted in the verdict. It is shown separately on the Impact step."
- **Step 3 section 6 "With the expected income"** (only when a raise is set; collapsed; the summary is the card's one line): "With the expected {amt} a month ({likely|uncertain}): {fits|needs adjustment|does not fit}, pays for itself in {n} months." If it never does within the horizon: "... does not pay for itself within {n} months." Expanded: the six numbers behind it (income with it, month left, payback month, total cost, state, how sure) in a two-column list. The main verdict never changes.
- **Setbacks runway lost:** in "If something goes wrong", the section summary becomes "Runway {n} months, {m} less because of this"; a `meta` line "Runway lost to this purchase: {m} months" under the note; each setback row gains a tag under its figure: "New payments still met" / "New payments cannot be met" (dot `accent` / `danger` plus the words).

### Running-cost prompts and the estimate gap (step 2, D-33, D-36)

- **Prompts:** "Costs that follow it" pre-lists, as unchecked `CheckRow`s with a blank amount field (placeholder "Add amount"), the categories the engine returns for the item type. Vehicle: Insurance, Fuel, Maintenance, Registration, Loss of value. A row counts only once an amount is typed ("Counted once an amount is typed" `meta`). **Loss of value (D-44, 04 D-63)** has no checkbox and takes the user's own entry: a segmented "A month" | "% a year" and one field (money input placeholder "Add amount", or `%` pill input placeholder "% a year"; the % is of the price before tax, fees and trade-ins, the advertised figure, not the final price), blank by default and never pre-filled. The row always shows `meta` "Not a monthly cash cost"; once an entry is typed it adds "About {x} a month. Shown here, not counted in your month." (the engine's non-cash figure, `RunningCosts.nonCash`). It never changes "Net monthly change", the month test or the verdict. Clearing the field removes it. Other types list their own categories; none ships with an amount.
- **Estimate gap (needs 3 or more decisions with actuals):** a `fill1` note (radius 16, padding `gapMd`, `meta` `inkDim`, no colour) under the "Costs that follow it" heading: "Your running costs have come in about {pct}% {higher|lower} than estimated." The same pattern appears once under the payment cards for the payment and day-one gaps ("Your payments have come in about {pct}% {higher|lower} than estimated"). Absent otherwise. Declarative, never an instruction.

### Savings goal for Wait (step 5, past decisions, D-34)

- **Wait creates one goal row** (target, monthly amount, ready-by date, saved so far from transfers the user tags to it). Phase 6 builds on the same table.
- **Progress bar:** `size.homeBarHeight` (8px), `radii.pill`, track `fill1`, fill `accent`, width animates with `DURATION.progress` (reduced motion: none). Caption beside it (`meta`): "{saved} of {target} saved". The bar is never the only signal; the caption and an `accessibilityValue` ("{pct} percent") carry it.
- **Where it shows:** the Wait panel on step 5 (a preview at 0), step 5 of the decided check when a Waiting check is opened, the Past decisions row (under the sub-line), and the decision detail sheet. Not in Open checks: a Waiting check is no longer open.
- **Tagging:** the detail sheet for a Waiting decision has "Tag a transfer" (secondary `Pill`) opening `AttachLineSheet` in its transfer mode (money-in transfer legs from the last 90 days not yet tagged; empty "No transfers in the last 90 days."; toast "Transfer tagged" with Undo); tagged transfers list like linked lines (name, date, amount, 44px "x" to untag, undo toast "Transfer untagged"). Saved so far is their sum.

### Cooling-off (step 5, D-35)

For checks whose size code is `needs_spreading`, step 5 shows a `ToggleRow` between the outcome cards and "What gets written": **"Check again in 48 hours"**, sub-copy "Reopens this check, recalculated. Off unless you turn it on." Default off. When on, the "What gets written" card adds "A reminder in 48 hours that reopens this check, recalculated." and the local notification fires 48 hours after commit (any outcome, including Pass). Notification text carries no amount or item name: title "Check ready", body "A check you saved is ready to look at again. Open it to see where it stands now." Notification permission flow as for Wait. Hidden for `from_spare_this_month` and `from_savings`.

### Currency conversion basis and missing rate (D-37)

- **Basis line:** see the basis detail row ("Includes amounts converted from EUR at 1.47 on 14 Oct"). The Assumptions section carries one row per converted currency (rate, date).
- **Missing rate:** when a needed rate is not stored the verdict is `missing`: chip "Missing terms", title "No rate is stored for {CUR}, so this cannot be scored yet.", body "Lines in {CUR} are waiting for a rate. Once one is stored, the check scores itself." and a text link "See lines waiting for a rate" (accent, 44 hit) that opens Activity, unfiltered (Activity has no waiting-for-a-rate filter in this phase; the lines show their own pending-rate state). No figure is guessed or shown. The quick check shows the same chip and line in its result block, with "Run the full check" disabled.

---

## Onboarding: first read (DCU-10, D-03, D-04)

Order: welcome, consent, one account and balance (`SetupAccountScreen`), **rough monthly in and out (new)**, **first check (new)**, then `SetupHistoryScreen` ("Bring your history"). Both new screens use the existing setup pattern: `Screen`, `SyncProblemLine`, a `heading` title (role header), `body` copy in `inkMuted`, 22px side padding, a bottom primary `Pill`. No progress bar (matches the existing screens).

**Rough in and out screen:** heading "Roughly, what comes in and goes out?"; body "Round figures are fine for a first look. Real months replace them." Two money fields in the account's currency: "Money in each month" and "Money out each month" (large `heading`-size inputs, `fill6` tiles, radius 16); primary **"See the first read"** (disabled until both are above 0); secondary text link "Skip for now" (the first check then answers only what cash alone can). Values are stored as the user's rough figures and are editable from Your month until a full month exists.

**First check screen:** heading "Try one thing you're thinking of buying"; body "A name and a price. This is a first look from the figures you gave."; fields "What is it?" and "Price"; button "Check it" (accent, 44+). **It uses the same inputs as the quick check (D-23):** the Cash | Credit toggle (Cash first), Credit-only APR (0%, "before any interest") and 12/24/36/48/60 chips, and the collapsed "+ Tax, fees" row, assessed by `firstRead()`. The result is the quick-check result block (chip, size label, line, monthly amount in Credit) with **no "Run the full check"** (the full check needs one full month, 04 D-11, so there is nothing to carry into): state chip (fits, "Needs adjustment" when cash would fall below 3 times the rough spending, "Not from cash"), size label, the one line, and a basis block (`fill1`, radius 16): **"First read, from the figures you gave. An import or a month of logging sharpens it."** Primary "Bring your history"; secondary "Check something else". Target: first verdict in about 60 seconds, about 7 taps and one typed line. Haptic on the result as step 3 (success/warning). No review prompt. Analytics `first_read_shown` fires with the state enum only.

---

## Copywriting Contract

Voice: declarative, never prescriptive. **No catalogue key under Decide may contain "advice", "recommend", "recommendation" or "you should"** (a test asserts it, DCU-09); Decide copy also avoids imperative instructions to the user in conclusions (the prototype lines "Ask for the written rate before signing", "Run the job-loss setback ... before you decide", "worth confirming those by hand", "Nothing else on this screen matters until that is fixed" are rewritten). Typographic apostrophes; all strings in `src/i18n/locales/en.ts`, marked draft in `copyStatus.ts`; every string in this file is Claude-drafted pending user review. Compliance framing: FincWin is a money-management tool, not a lender or a financial institution; the card path must never read as an offer.

**Amended 2026-10-22 (review WR-D04, WR-B09, IN-D05; user-accepted):** the DCU-09 guard (`src/i18n/__tests__/decideCopy.test.ts`) now also fails reassurance about the future ("safe to", "don't worry", "no problem", "go for it", "you can afford", "worth it", "safely" and the like) anywhere in Decide copy, and Decide copy that opens with a money imperative (Spend, Save, Try, Keep, Buy, Borrow, Skip, Put, Cut, Pay, Invest, Sell, Stop) outside a keyed list of buttons and field captions. That guard drove these rewrites, reflected in the tables below: "Try on credit" → "See it on credit"; "Spend less, keep the rest working" → "Cheaper, and what it leaves" (shortened 2026-10-23 from "The cheaper one, and what it leaves", 35 characters, over the rule E cap) with the sub-line "The difference stays yours for the whole {n} months."; "Save first, then buy" → "Saving first, then buying"; "Skip it entirely" → "Not buying it"; "Put it on a card" → "On a card"; "Put aside a month" → "Set aside each month"; "Buy at month {n}, then keep the {pay} a month" → "Bought at month {n}, then {pay} a month kept"; the steady line → "{n} months of income within {pct} of each other, so the check uses the average." The third card note keeps its polarity and loses the insurance framing: "Is something set aside to keep these payments going if income dips?".

---

## Interaction states and motion

- **Motion (all through `src/theme/motion.ts`, reduced motion collapses them):** flow push in/out `fw-push` (`DURATION.push` 240); step change content `fw-rise` (`DURATION.entrance` 220); verdict card reveal `fw-rise`; section expand `fw-rise`; quick result `fw-rise`; sheets per 03. The step bar and step change do not slide. No new motion token.
- **Pressed states:** tappable rows and links `PRESSED_OPACITY` (0.72); pills darken to `ink` where the prototype did.
- **Haptics (the complete list, via `src/ui/haptics.ts`, async API only):** first verdict reveal `notificationAsync` (Success for fits, Warning otherwise); choosing a method, chip, outcome or step segment `selectionAsync`; committing Go ahead or Wait `impactAsync` Medium (Pass: selection only, no commit haptic); a full swipe on a `SwipeRow` (Open checks, Past decisions, Changes coming up) `hapticLight` = `impactAsync` Light (shipped); fold rows, Details toggles and ⓘ `selectionAsync` (R5, amended 2026-10-22); nothing on text entry.
- **Loading:** skeleton bars (`fill1`, final size, no shimmer) in the quick-check result, Your month, and the verdict card position; container `accessibilityState.busy`.
- **Offline:** everything computes from the cached snapshot; edits queue; the sync line shows the state. Go ahead works offline through the write queue.
- **Recompute (D-56, user 2026-10-16):** every keystroke recomputes the verdict card, the warnings, the step 2 method cards and the quick-check result at once; the step 3 sections, step 4 (alternatives and investing) and the other detail figures recompute 150 ms after typing stops (no spinner; the settling sections are `accessibilityState.busy`). A snapshot change recomputes both at once. If the device check still shows lag, step 4 and the investing views compute only while visible (05-30 Task 2, conditional). The verdict card is a polite live region.

---

## Accessibility

- 44px minimum targets (`hitSlop` for checkboxes, "x", segments, thumbs, chips).
- Radio groups (`ChoiceCard`) use `role="radio"`/`checked`; `CheckRow` uses `role="checkbox"`/`checked`; collapsible headers expose `expanded`; sliders are `adjustable` with increment/decrement; the step bar segments are `button`s with `disabled` when unreached.
- Reading order step 3: header, verdict card (one element), notices, warnings (each a group "{title}, {value}. {description}"), sections.
- Colour is never the only signal: state chips carry words; over/under carry "over/under/short"; pass/fail dots sit beside "Clear"/"Short"/"Over"; the changed marker is text; the dark `danger` verdict card is reinforced by the chip words "Doesn’t fit right now".
- Text scaling: body and labels to 200% wrapping or stacking; verdict title, strip figures, Final price, Cheaper-one price cap 1.3x; strips stack above 1.3x. Test at the largest OS sizes and at 320pt wide.
- Contrast: all new pairings are existing token pairs; the one new fill/text pair (`surface` on `danger`) is 5.4:1.

---

## Analytics (ANL-06, D-20)

Events through `src/services/analytics/catalogue.ts` (no amounts, no names): `quick_check_run` {state: fits|adjust|no|missing}; `check_started`; `check_step_viewed` {step: 1..5}; `check_completed` {state}; `check_abandoned`; `decision_recorded` {decision: go|wait|pass}; `first_read_shown` {state}. `check_completed` fires when step 3's verdict is first shown for a check; `check_step_viewed` once per step per visit. The size code, method and kind are not sent.

## Demo account and screenshot readiness (DCU-11, D-19)

Seeded as the demo account's sample months plus two open checks, in the real app (nothing staged). Personal scope; the scope toggle is hidden; the sync line absent when online.

| Frame | Screen state | Must be visible without scrolling at 402 x 874 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 "Can I afford it? Ask first." | Step 3 of the sofa check ($1,800, paid in full) | Flow header, "It fits" chip, size label, verdict title, body, basis marker; first warning area empty (no warnings) so the first collapsed section header is visible below |
| 2 "See what breaks before you buy" | Step 3 of the car check ($14,500 on a loan, APR typed in the seed as a user would) | "Needs adjustment" chip, size label "Needs spreading", title, body, and **two warning cards fully visible** (they end at or above y = 703, or 728 with the basis detail row, above the bar at y = 735) |
| 3 "Cheaper, later, or skip it" | Step 4 of the car check | The card heading and **all four option rows**, each with name, On the day, A month and Kept (or "—" with its one-line reason). Reason lines count toward the budget; As planned has none, so at most three (fourth row ends at or above y = 748) |

**Depth-surface constraint for the seed:** no one-offs, no foreign-currency lines, confirmed pay series present (so the cash path draws), no dated future changes, no `on_edge` lines, confidence "Strong" or "Fair" is acceptable, **no cash dip without the purchase (so no `cash_tight_before_payday` fact card; there are no Notices cards in frames 1-3)**, and the car check's combined state with the sofa equals its standalone state. This keeps the optional basis detail row and the edge tag out of frames 1-3 and the budgets unchanged.

Alternate frame 1 capture: Decide home with "sofa 1800" run in the quick check; the result card must be legible at 402 x 874 as budgeted. No rate appears that the user did not enter; the loan's APR is seed data typed into the check. The legibility floor is the scale: nothing below 10.5px, body at 13-15px, contrast as above.

## Deviations Register

| # | Deviation | Prototype | This build | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Quick check position | Below Your month | Above Your month | Fold budget; the public promise first |
| 2 | Quick check | Cash-only, "Not from cash" + hardcoded 9.4%/48-month sentence | Always-present Cash/Credit toggle; Credit APR starts at 0% "before any interest"; 12-60 month chips; collapsed "+ Tax, fees"; both modes through `assess()`; "See it on credit" when cash falls short | D-02, D-23 |
| 3 | Size label | none | Eyebrow/chip from the engine size code | D-22 |
| 4 | Step 3 layout | Month table first, verdict card lead with all content open | Verdict, notices, warnings, then up to nine collapsed sections with summaries (the prototype's seven plus "Day by day" and "With the expected income"; the personal-scope demo shows seven) | D-05, D-26, D-32 |
| 5 | Step 3 pause case | `inv` mirrors `month` | Amber `inv` card stating the amount that pauses | D-06, 04 D-35 |
| 6 | Verdict card `no` | `#5A2318` | `danger` fill | No new colour |
| 7 | Dark-card inner fills, value tints, warning amber | many hex values | `surface`@.12 over `ink`; `accentTint1`/`dangerTint2`; `categoryTint.Business` + `warn1` | No new colour |
| 8 | Fits chip | fixed green tint | `accent` at `ACCENT_TINT_OPACITY` over `fill1` | Accent is user-themeable (03 precedent) |
| 9 | Alternatives table | 4-column grid, 134px name column | Four stacked option rows with a 3-column strip | Legibility at 402 (DCU-11) |
| 10 | Step 4 order | Slider card, table, trade | Table, slider card, trade | Screenshot 3 budget |
| 11 | "If saved" / "keeps earning" | 6.5% return field | "Kept", 0% default, "+ Add a rate" | D-07, 04 D-06 |
| 12 | Date picker | Custom month grid sheet | Existing native `DateField` (`minDate` today) + three offset chips | D-13 |
| 13 | Offset chips | +7 / +30 / +90 days | +7 days, +1 calendar month, +3 calendar months (day clamped) | Researcher assumption |
| 14 | Pass copy | "Filed for twelve months" / "Kept on file until" | "Kept until you delete it", no reminders, reopenable | D-11 |
| 15 | Estimate-vs-actual | Static seed data | Linked lines at 30 and 90 days | D-12 |
| 16 | Go ahead | writes directly | One confirm sheet, one undo step | D-09 |
| 17 | Open checks | "Restore both" | Removed; abandon has an undo toast | D-15, DCU-04 |
| 18 | Card questions and `ex` warnings | Drive extra warnings | Kept as neutral notes; no warning effect | Compliance, D-05 five-warning rule |
| 19 | Action bar | At the end of the scroll | Pinned above the keyboard/safe area | Reachability |
| 20 | Radii / spacing / type | 24/22/20/18/14, 12/14 gaps, 10-30px sizes | 26/16/pill, `space` tokens, closed scale | 03 precedent |
| 21 | Sliders | HTML range | Gesture-handler `StepSlider`, no package | Stack |
| 22 | Prescriptive strings | "Ask for the written rate", "Run the job-loss setback", "worth confirming by hand", "Nothing else on this screen matters" | Rewritten declaratively | DCU-09 |
| 23 | Wait notification | none | Local reminder, no amounts or item name | D-10, privacy |
| 24 | Wait | No goal object | A minimal savings goal row with a progress bar | D-34 |
| 25 | Step 3 sections | seven | up to nine (adds Day by day, always; With the expected income, only with a raise; Who pays what stays household-only) | D-26, D-32 |
| 26 | Combined-checks control | none | Pill in the flow header and the quick-check footer | D-24 |
| 27 | Investing | "keeps earning" at a 6.5% default | Four views, 0% plus up to three user rates, labelled "not a forecast" | D-31, 04 D-51 |
| 28 | Warning cap | five | up to six (`cash_dip`) | 04 D-44 |

## Registry Safety

Not applicable. React Native/Expo project: no shadcn registry, no `components.json`, no third-party component blocks. One package is added: `expo-notifications` (SDK-locked, from the project's own stack list). No slider package.

| Registry | Blocks Used | Safety Gate |
|----------|-------------|-------------|
| shadcn official | none (not applicable to RN) | not applicable |
| third-party | none | not applicable |

## Researcher assumptions (for your review)

Each item is a choice I made instead of asking, picking the option most consistent with CONTEXT, the prototype and the 03/03.1 specs.

1. **Quick check moves above Your month**, and the stacked option rows replace the alternatives grid, and step 4 shows the table before the slider, all to meet the DCU-11 fold budgets. The prototype order is otherwise kept.
2. **All step-3 sections (up to nine; seven in the personal-scope demo) are collapsed by default**, each with a one-line summary, so the verdict and warnings fill the screenshot frames. The alternative is leaving "The month, line by line" open.
3. **A check autosaves on every edit**; "Save" means save and close; leaving never prompts. Abandon has no confirm, an Undo toast instead.
4. **Dark cards:** `ink`, with `danger` for the `no` verdict card (white on `#B4472A` is 5.4:1). Inner dark fills are `surface` at 0.12 opacity.
5. **Quick-check "no" chip says "Not from cash" in Cash mode and "Not from credit" in Credit mode**, not "Doesn’t fit right now", because each quick check judges one payment route and another may still work. The first read uses the same D-23 inputs (toggle, APR, duration chips, tax and fees) through `firstRead()`; it only lacks "Run the full check".
6. **Label rule:** "before any interest" shows whenever the Credit APR is 0, not only while untouched, so it can never be read as a quoted rate. Duration chip "12" is selected on first show. "See it on credit" keeps the amount, tax and fees and starts APR at 0.
7. **First read has no "Run the full check"** (the full verdict needs a full month); its Credit mode is scored by the cash-level `firstRead()` only. Skipping the rough figures is allowed and gives a cash-only answer.
8. **Offset chips** are +7 days, +1 calendar month and +3 calendar months (prototype: +7/+30/+90 days).
9. **Card questions** are kept as neutral notes with no verdict effect; the prototype's question-driven warning cards are dropped. Say if you want the questions removed altogether.
10. **Worst/Average basis chips** appear only with 3 or more full months and the choice is stored with the limits; below that the basis is fixed by the engine rule (04 D-12).
11. **Unsecured-debt line is a fifth editable row** in "Lines you will not cross" (04 D-04 made it editable).
12. **Display rounding (amended by D-54, user 2026-10-16):** monthly payments, instalments and promotion payments show cents everywhere (the quick-check monthly figure, the method cards' "A month", the alternatives "A month", the plan card, the promo and stress lines, "Set aside each month"); large totals and balances (prices, cash, "Kept", interest, totals, debts) stay whole currency units; the month line-by-line table and the "What gets written" list show exact minor units so rows sum. Fold check: a cents figure adds three characters and stays one line in every strip and in the Row D `heading` figure at 402 (a $12,345.67 strip figure is about 86 of the 104 dp a column has), so every budget holds. The engine is unaffected.
13. **Wait reminder text carries no amount and no item name** because notifications show on the lock screen.
14. **Removing a decision does not delete the lines it wrote.**
15. **Engine code names:** only `card_not_accepted`, `card_apr_missing`, `debt_rate_exceeds_return`, the size codes and warning codes `month`/`cash`/`dsr`/`owe`/`inv` are named in 04-CONTEXT. Other reason and suppression codes are referred to by meaning here; the planner maps them to the final engine barrel names.
16. **`expo-notifications` is the only added dependency**, and the slider is hand-built.
17. **One rate list per check** (0% plus up to three user rates) feeds both the investing-instead section and the "Kept" column; the Kept column and the alternatives table use the first rate in the list, or 0% if none.
18. **Combined-checks pill lives in the flow header (all steps) and the quick-check footer** rather than as its own row, so DCU-11 holds; it is on by default and remembered per check. The demo seed must keep the car check "Needs adjustment" when combined with the sofa; frame 1 (sofa) is captured with the toggle off (05 D-24 amended).
19. **Verdict body line 1 is the difference line** (combined first, then worst-month); other basis facts (one-offs, conversion, cash path unavailable) share one optional `meta` row under the card, and overflow goes to "Assumptions in play". The demo seed has none of them.
20. **"On the edge" shows as a tag by the state chip** with the margin text in "Against your lines" and the chip's accessible name, because the chip row cannot hold the margin sentence at 402.
21. **Confidence chip** sits in verdict row 2 beside the size label; labels are "Strong / Fair / Rough basis".
22. **Warning descriptions are capped at 3 lines in the DCU-11 frames** (down from 4) so the cash-path and confidence additions still fit; longer text scrolls only above 1.3x text scale.
23. **Changes coming up** sits between Open checks and Past decisions; the start/end pickers reuse `DateField` showing month and year; stored per user.
24. **Wait now creates a goal row** (D-34 amends D-10); tagging transfers is done from the decision detail sheet through `AttachLineSheet`'s transfer mode, with Record's transfer detail untouched in this phase.
25. **Cooling-off applies to any outcome** (including Pass) when the size code is `needs_spreading`, reusing the generic reminder text.
26. **Chart construction** (cash path and net worth) is hand-built in `react-native-svg` with solid vs dashed lines as the second cue and a text alternative; no charting package is added.
27. **Missing-rate result** disables "Run the full check" in the quick check and offers a link that opens Activity (unfiltered in this phase).

## Alignment to the executed Phase 4 (2026-10-16)

Applied per 05-CONTEXT D-45..D-52 after Phase 4 was executed, reviewed (04-REVIEW) and verified. Where an earlier line of this spec and the engine code disagree, the code in `src/engine/decide` and `src/engine/payoff` wins. Status stays approved; the DCU-11 fold budgets, the frames and the declarative voice are unchanged (every new line fits an existing slot or sits below the fold, and the demo seed reaches none of the new states).

1. **Term and payoff month (D-45, 04 D-64 / ME-03):** level payments round up to the cent, so a plan can be paid off before the term typed. Every term display shows the term typed; the payoff month is stated beside it only when it is earlier: the quick check caption "a month for {n} months · paid off in month {m}" (one `meta` line, budget unchanged), the step 2 loan line "Paid off in month {m}, with a last payment of {x}.", the card "Cleared" tile (months actually paid). "Four ways, same {n} months" and the investing horizon follow the engine's months actually paid. "What gets written" follows the engine's payment runs. Sections: Quick check (Row D), Step 2 (Loan, Card), Step 4 item 1, Step 5 Go ahead, Investing instead (Horizon), Copywriting "Quick check Credit fields".
2. **Card minimum open at 50 years (D-46, 04 HI-02):** the engine no longer reports a cleared total or interest for it; the tiles and "Total paid" read "Not within 50 years", the note reads "Still owing {x} after 50 years at the minimum." and the step 3 card reason states "{x} left". It stays distinct from "Never" / "Endless" (a minimum that never clears). Sections: Step 2 (payment cards, Card), Step 3 card reasons.
3. **Projections out of range (D-47, 04 ME-01):** a kept figure, a "Without it" figure, an investing-view figure or a net-worth point the engine cannot compute exactly reads "Too large to project at this rate"; the chart skips those points (never drawn as 0) and its text alternative says so. Step 4 keeps one reason line per row. Sections: Step 4 (Suppression), section 8 (options), Investing instead.
4. **Rate bound (D-48, 04 ME-01 `MAX_PROJECTION_RATE`):** every projection-rate field accepts up to 100% a year and refuses more with the hint "Up to 100% a year"; the engine code `projection_rate_out_of_range` has a title for completeness. Sections: Step 4 (Return control), Investing instead (Rates), Step 3 title table.
5. **Instalment terms (D-49, 04 ME-02 / LO-05 / HI-03):** on-the-day fees are paid on the day and the instalments cover the rest (a note variant on the amount financed); instalments that add up to less than they must cover are "Below the price", with a note asking for the amount or the count; "Use this price" and "Use the cheaper price" rescale an instalment plan (and the cheaper row a loan deposit) exactly as the engine did, so the rescored check is the one shown; the cheaper row's descriptor names what was scaled. Sections: Step 2 (Instalment, flags), Step 3 title table and section 8, Step 4 (descriptor, trade line).
6. **No money in (D-50, 04 ME-05 / LO-01):** with an income basis of 0 any debt payment is over the debt line and the ratio cannot be worked out, so the quick line, the verdict title, the dsr warning and the Debt service row use their no-money-in wording, never a blank or "null%"; a history with no money in reads "No money in is logged in these {n} months, so every check is judged on that." on Your month. Sections: Quick check table, Your month, Step 3 title and warnings tables, section 5.
7. **Promotion balance (D-52):** "Left when it ends: {x}" is the balance after the promotion's last month, from the same payment schedule the engine uses (the engine's promotion summary has no such field). Section: Credit terms in step 2.
8. **Copywriting Contract:** new row "Engine states (alignment 2026-10-16)" and additions to "Quick check Credit fields" and "Credit result". Every new string passes the banned list; the new verdict titles are 59, 55 and 46 characters and every missing-state title is at most 64 (cap 64, D-55), and the new reason line is 33 (cap 44).
9. **No change needed (checked against the code):** one-off refunds now net against one-off spending (the basis line shows only the count); on-the-day fees are clamped to the price; the snapshot builder validates limits before calling the engine (no UI); the D-59..D-63 names in this spec match the barrel.

Status stays approved. The DCU-11 budgets are unchanged (every new line replaces or joins an existing slot; cents add three characters to a one-line figure).

1. **Verdicts led by `cash_dip` (plan-check blocker):** warnings are ordered by shortfall, so the dip can lead an `adjust`, and a hard dip alone makes the state `no` even when cash stays above 0 on the day and the month closes. New titles "Your cash dips {x} under your cushion before payday." and "Your cash would run out before money comes in."; new quick lines for Cash led by `cash_dip`, `dsr` or `owe`, Credit led by `cash_dip` (soft and hard), and Cash `no` without a cash warning. Precedence for `no`: cash below 0, then the month, then a hard dip; every warning code has a title and a quick line. Sections: Quick check table, Step 3 title table, Copywriting "Engine states".
2. **Missing-state titles (UI-check F1, D-55):** every one is at most 64 characters; the long ones are shortened ("can’t be scored"). The catalogue test asserts it. Section: Step 3 title table.
3. **Card wording in the contract (F2):** "Not within 50 years", "Not cleared within 50 years", "Never" and "Endless" are listed in the "Engine states" and "Card costs" rows.
4. **dsr value with no money in (F3):** "None in".
5. **0% caption (F4):** at 0% the Credit caption is exactly "a month for {n} months, before any interest" with no payoff suffix; the suffix needs a rate above 0 and something financed.
6. **D-53:** "To clear it inside the promotion: {x} a month" is the first promotion tile. Sections: Credit terms in step 2, Copywriting "Credit terms".
7. **D-54:** monthly payments, instalments and promotion payments show cents; totals and balances stay whole (assumption 12 amended, fold check recorded there).
8. **D-56:** the verdict, warnings, method cards and quick result recompute on every keystroke; the sections, step 4 and the investing views 150 ms after typing stops, with a conditional lazy fallback. Section: Interaction states and motion.
9. Card fees are now written at Go ahead as part of the day-one cash line; an instalment's written lines start with the day-one line (first instalment and fees). Section: Step 5 "What gets written".
